              (РїСЂР°РІР°СЏ С€РєР°Р»Р°). Р¦РёС„СЂС‹ СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ СЃС‚РѕР»Р±С†РѕРІ.
            </p>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="h-80">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <BarChart data={mergedDaily} margin={{ left: 8, right: 8, top: 28 }}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" className="stroke-muted" />
                <XAxis
                  dataKey="date"
                  tick={{ fontSize: 11 }}
                  tickFormatter={(v) => formatAxisDay(String(v))}
                />
                <YAxis yAxisId="left" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} allowDecimals={false} />
                <YAxis
                  yAxisId="right"
                  orientation="right"
                  tick={{ fontSize: 11 }}
                  tickFormatter={(v) =>
                    Number(v) >= 1000 ? `${(Number(v) / 1000).toFixed(1)}k` : String(v)
                  }
                />
                <Tooltip
                  labelFormatter={(label) => `Р”Р°С‚Р°: ${formatAxisDay(String(label))}`}
                  formatter={(
                    value: number,
                    _name: string,
                    item: { dataKey?: string }
                  ) => {
                    const key = item?.dataKey;
                    if (key === "revenue")
                      return [`${value} в‚Ѕ`, "Р’С‹СЂСѓС‡РєР°"];
                    if (key === "users") return [value, "Р РµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёРё"];
                    if (key === "tickets") return [value, "Р‘РёР»РµС‚С‹"];
                    return [value, _name];
                  }}
                />
                <Legend />
                <Bar
                  yAxisId="left"
                  dataKey="users"
                  name="Р РµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёРё"
                  fill="#2563eb"
                  radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                  maxBarSize={36}
                >
                  <LabelList
                    dataKey="users"
                    position="top"
                    className="fill-blue-700 text-[10px] font-medium"
                    formatter={(v: number) => (v > 0 ? String(v) : "")}
                  />
                </Bar>
                <Bar
                  yAxisId="left"
                  dataKey="tickets"
                  name="Р‘РёР»РµС‚С‹"
                  fill="#16a34a"
                  radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                  maxBarSize={36}
                >
                  <LabelList
                    dataKey="tickets"
                    position="top"
                    className="fill-green-700 text-[10px] font-medium"
                    formatter={(v: number) => (v > 0 ? String(v) : "")}
                  />
                </Bar>
                <Bar
                  yAxisId="right"
                  dataKey="revenue"
                  name="Р’С‹СЂСѓС‡РєР° в‚Ѕ"
                  fill="#c026d3"
                  radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                  maxBarSize={36}
                >
                  <LabelList
                    dataKey="revenue"
                    position="top"
                    className="fill-fuchsia-800 text-[10px] font-medium"
                    formatter={(v: number) =>
                      v > 0 ? `${Number(v).toLocaleString("ru-RU")}` : ""
                    }
                  />

              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Р—Р° РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґ РЅРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РґР»СЏ РіСЂР°С„РёРєР°.
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";

function Field({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  multiline,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  multiline?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1.5">
      <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</Label>
      {multiline ? (
        <Textarea value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} rows={3} />
      ) : (
        <Input value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function MediaField({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  accept,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  accept: string;
}) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1.5">
      <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</Label>
      <Input
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="URL РёР»Рё Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚Рµ С„Р°Р№Р»"
      />
      <label className="inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-xs text-primary">
        <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
        {busy ? "Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°вЂ¦" : "Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р»"}
        <input
          type="file"
          accept={accept}
          className="hidden"
          disabled={busy}
          onChange={async (e) => {
            const file = e.target.files?.[0];
            if (!file) return;
            setBusy(true);
            try {
              const urls = await uploadImages([file]);
              if (urls[0]) onChange(urls[0]);
            } catch (err) {
              console.error(err);
              alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё");
            } finally {
              setBusy(false);
              e.target.value = "";
            }
          }}
        />
      </label>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Double-click header to rename (updates the underlying string). */
function DblRename({
  value,
  onChange,
  hint = "Р”РІРѕР№РЅРѕР№ РєР»РёРє вЂ” РїРµСЂРµРёРјРµРЅРѕРІР°С‚СЊ",
}: {
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  hint?: string;
}) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editing) setDraft(value);
  }, [value, editing]);

  if (editing) {
    return (
      <Input
        autoFocus
        value={draft}
        className="h-7 text-xs font-semibold"
        onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
        onBlur={() => {
          onChange(draft.trim() || value);
          setEditing(false);
        }}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === "Enter") {
            onChange(draft.trim() || value);
            setEditing(false);
          }
          if (e.key === "Escape") setEditing(false);
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <p
      title={hint}
      onDoubleClick={() => {
        setDraft(value);
        setEditing(true);
      }}
      className="cursor-text rounded px-0.5 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-muted"
    >
      {value || "Р‘РµР· РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ"}
    </p>
  );
}

function patchItem(
  items: unknown[],
  i: number,
  patch: Record<string, unknown>,
  onPatch: (p: Record<string, unknown>) => void,
  key = "items"
) {
  const next = [...items] as Record<string, unknown>[];
  next[i] = { ...(next[i] as object), ...patch };
  onPatch({ [key]: next });
}

/**
 * РџР°СЂСЃРёС‚ С‡РёСЃР»Рѕ РёР· РїРѕР»СЏ РІРІРѕРґР°. РџСѓСЃС‚РѕРµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ в†’ 0.
 * Р’РђР–РќРћ: РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ `Number(v) || 0`, РёРЅР°С‡Рµ РІРІРµРґС‘РЅРЅС‹Р№ РЅРѕР»СЊ
 * РїСЂРµРІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ NaN/РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ Рё СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ РЅР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Рµ В«СЃРєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏВ».
 */
function toNumberOrZero(v: string): number {
  const n = Number(String(v).replace(/\s+/g, "").replace(",", "."));
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}

function DayItemLinkPicker({
  kind,
  refId,
  onPick,
}: {
  kind: string;
  refId: string;
  onPick: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
}) {
  const routesQ = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin-routes-picker"],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<AdminRouteRow[]>("/api/admin/routes"),
  });
  const blogQ = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin-blog-picker"],
    queryFn: () => apiFetch<AdminBlogRow[]>("/api/admin/blog/posts"),
  });

  const mode = kind === "route" || kind === "blog" ? kind : "custom";

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2 rounded-md bg-muted/40 p-2">
      <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">РџСЂРёРІСЏР·РєР°</Label>
      <Select
        value={mode}
        onValueChange={(v) => {
          if (v === "custom") {
            onPick({ kind: "custom", refId: "" });
          } else {
            onPick({ kind: v, refId: "" });
          }
        }}
      >
        <SelectTrigger className="h-8">
          <SelectValue />
        </SelectTrigger>
        <SelectContent>
          <SelectItem value="custom">РЎРІРѕСЏ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєР°</SelectItem>
          <SelectItem value="route">РњР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚ РёР· CMS</SelectItem>
          <SelectItem value="blog">РЎС‚Р°С‚СЊСЏ Р±Р»РѕРіР°</SelectItem>
        </SelectContent>
      </Select>

      {mode === "route" && (
        <Select
          value={refId || undefined}
          onValueChange={(id) => {
            const row = (routesQ.data || []).find((r) => r.id === id);
            if (!row) return;
            const pathId = row.legacy_id || row.slug || row.id;
            onPick({
              kind: "route",
              refId: row.id,
              name: row.name,
              href: `/routes/${pathId}`,
              rating: row.rating ?? 0,
              image: row.cover_image_url || "",
            });
          }}
        >
          <SelectTrigger className="h-8">
            <SelectValue placeholder={routesQ.isLoading ? "Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°вЂ¦" : "Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚"} />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            {(routesQ.data || []).map((r) => (
              <SelectItem key={r.id} value={r.id}>
                {r.name}
                {r.status === "published" ? "" : " (С‡РµСЂРЅРѕРІРёРє)"}
              </SelectItem>
            ))}
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
      )}

      {mode === "blog" && (
        <Select
          value={refId || undefined}
          onValueChange={(id) => {
            const row = (blogQ.data || []).find((r) => r.id === id);
            if (!row) return;
            onPick({
              kind: "blog",
              refId: row.id,
              name: row.title,
              href: `/blog/${row.slug}`,
              image: row.cover_image_url || "",
            });
          }}
        >
          <SelectTrigger className="h-8">
            <SelectValue placeholder={blogQ.isLoading ? "Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°вЂ¦" : "Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЃС‚Р°С‚СЊСЋ"} />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            {(blogQ.data || []).map((r) => (
              <SelectItem key={r.id} value={r.id}>
                {r.title}
              </SelectItem>
            ))}
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
      )}

      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground leading-snug">
        Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚ РёР»Рё СЃС‚Р°С‚СЊСЋ вЂ” РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ, СЃСЃС‹Р»РєР° Рё С„РѕС‚Рѕ РїРѕРґСЃС‚Р°РІСЏС‚СЃСЏ СЃР°РјРё.
        Р›РёР±Рѕ СЂРµР¶РёРј В«РЎРІРѕСЏ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєР°В» Рё СѓРєР°Р¶РёС‚Рµ URL РІСЂСѓС‡РЅСѓСЋ.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function ArticlePicker({
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: string[];
  onChange: (ids: string[]) => void;
}) {
  const postsQ = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin-blog-posts-news-picker"],
    queryFn: () =>
      apiFetch<AdminBlogRow[]>("/api/admin/blog/posts"),
  });

  const posts = postsQ.data || [];
  const selected = value.slice(0, 10);

  const toggle = (id: string) => {
    if (selected.includes(id)) {
      onChange(selected.filter((x) => x !== id));
    } else {
      if (selected.length >= 10) return;
      onChange([...selected, id]);
    }
  };

  const move = (id: string, dir: -1 | 1) => {
    const idx = selected.indexOf(id);
    if (idx < 0) return;
    const next = [...selected];
    const j = idx + dir;
    if (j < 0 || j >= next.length) return;
    [next[idx], next[j]] = [next[j], next[idx]];
    onChange(next);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2 rounded-md border p-3">
      <Label className="text-sm font-semibold">
        РЎС‚Р°С‚СЊРё РІ СЃР»Р°Р№РґРµСЂРµ ({selected.length}/10)
      </Label>
      <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
        Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РґРѕ 10 СЃС‚Р°С‚РµР№. Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРµ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРѕ РЅРё РѕРґРЅРѕР№ вЂ” РїРѕРєР°Р¶РµРј 6 РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёС….
      </p>

      {selected.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="space-y-1">
          {selected.map((id, i) => {
            const p = posts.find((x) => x.id === id);
            return (
              <li
                key={id}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded border bg-muted/30 px-2 py-1 text-xs"
              >
                <span className="flex-1 truncate">{p?.title || id}</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="px-1 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground disabled:opacity-30"
                  onClick={() => move(id, -1)}
                  disabled={i === 0}
                  title="Р’С‹С€Рµ"
                >
                  в†‘
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="px-1 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground disabled:opacity-30"
                  onClick={() => move(id, 1)}
                  disabled={i === selected.length - 1}
                  title="РќРёР¶Рµ"
                >
                  в†“
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="px-1 text-destructive"
                  onClick={() => toggle(id)}
                  title="РЈР±СЂР°С‚СЊ"
                >
                  вњ•
                </button>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}

      <div className="max-h-64 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto rounded border p-2">
        {postsQ.isLoading ? (
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°вЂ¦</p>
        ) : posts.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">РќРµС‚ СЃС‚Р°С‚РµР№</p>
        ) : (
          posts.map((p) => {
            const isSelected = selected.includes(p.id);
            const isDisabled = !isSelected && selected.length >= 10;
            return (
              <label
                key={p.id}
                className={cn(
                  "flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded p-1 text-xs hover:bg-muted",
                  isDisabled && "cursor-not-allowed opacity-50",
                )}
              >
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={isSelected}
                  disabled={isDisabled}
                  onChange={() => toggle(p.id)}
                />
                <span className="flex-1 truncate">{p.title}</span>
                {p.status && p.status !== "published" ? (
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
                    {p.status === "draft" ? "С‡РµСЂРЅРѕРІРёРє" : p.status}
                  </span>
                ) : null}
              </label>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function AdminPageSectionFields({
  sectionId,
  fields,
  onPatch,
}: {
  sectionId: string;
  fields: Record<string, unknown>;
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
}) {
  const str = (key: string) => String(fields[key] ?? "");

  if (sectionId === "hero") {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Field label="Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("title")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ title: v })} />
        <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={str("titleColor")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ titleColor: v })} />
        <MediaField label="Р’РёРґРµРѕ: РїРѕРµР·Рґ" value={str("videoTrain")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ videoTrain: v })} accept="video/mp4,video/webm" />
        <MediaField label="Р’РёРґРµРѕ: СЃР°РјРѕР»С‘С‚" value={str("videoFlight")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ videoFlight: v })} accept="video/mp4,video/webm" />
        <MediaField label="Р’РёРґРµРѕ: Р°РІС‚РѕР±СѓСЃ" value={str("videoBus")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ videoBus: v })} accept="video/mp4,video/webm" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (sectionId === "features") {
    const items = Array.isArray(fields.items) ? fields.items : [];
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Field label="Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("title")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ title: v })} />
        <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={str("titleColor")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ titleColor: v })} />
        <Field label="РџРѕРґР·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("subtitle")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ subtitle: v })} multiline />
        <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ РїРѕРґР·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={str("subtitleColor")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ subtitleColor: v })} />
        {items.map((raw, i) => {
          const it = raw as Record<string, string>;
          return (
            <div key={i} className="space-y-2 rounded-md border p-3">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">РљР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєР° {i + 1}</p>
              <DblRename
                value={it.title || `РљР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєР° ${i + 1}`}
                onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { title: v }, onPatch)}
              />
              <Field label="Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={it.title || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { title: v }, onPatch)} />
              <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={it.titleColor || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { titleColor: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ" value={it.description || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { description: v }, onPatch)} multiline />
              <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёСЏ" value={it.descriptionColor || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { descriptionColor: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РџРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚ (РјРѕРґР°Р»РєР°)" value={it.fullDescription || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { fullDescription: v }, onPatch)} multiline />
              <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ С„РѕРЅР° РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРё" value={it.bgColor || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { bgColor: v }, onPatch)} />
              <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ РёРєРѕРЅРєРё" value={it.iconBgColor || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { iconBgColor: v }, onPatch)} />
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (sectionId === "recommendedTrains") {
    const items = Array.isArray(fields.items) ? fields.items : [];
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Field label="Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("title")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ title: v })} />

        <Field label="РџРѕРґР·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("subtitle")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ subtitle: v })} multiline />

        {items.map((raw, i) => {

                  if (parts.length >= 2) {
                    patchItem(items, i, {
                      from: parts[0].trim(),

                    }, onPatch);
                  } else {
                    patchItem(items, i, { from: v.trim() }, onPatch);
                  }
                }}

              <Field label="РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ" value={String(it.description || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { description: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РўРµРі" value={String(it.tag || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { tag: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РЎРєРёРґРєР° %" value={String(it.discount ?? "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { discount: toNumberOrZero(v) }, onPatch)} />

              <Field label="РќРѕРІР°СЏ С†РµРЅР°" value={String(it.newPrice || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { newPrice: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РЎСЃС‹Р»РєР° (РѕРїС†.)" value={String(it.href || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { href: v }, onPatch)} />
              <MediaField label="Р¤РѕС‚Рѕ" value={String(it.image || "")} accept="image/*" onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { image: v }, onPatch)} />
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (sectionId === "routesSection") {
    const routes = Array.isArray(fields.routes) ? fields.routes : [];
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Field label="Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("title")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ title: v })} />
        <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={str("titleColor")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ titleColor: v })} />
        <Field label="РџРѕРґР·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("subtitle")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ subtitle: v })} multiline />
        <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ РїРѕРґР·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={str("subtitleColor")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ subtitleColor: v })} />
        {routes.map((raw, i) => {
          const it = raw as Record<string, string | number | boolean>;
          const pairLabel = `${String(it.from || "")} в†’ ${String(it.to || "")}`;
          return (
            <div key={i} className="space-y-2 rounded-md border p-3">
              <DblRename
                value={pairLabel}
                onChange={(v) => {
                  const parts = v.split(/\s*в†’\s*|\s*->\s*/);
                  if (parts.length >= 2) {
                    patchItem(
                      routes,

                      { from: parts[0].trim(), to: parts.slice(1).join("в†’").trim() },

                    );
                  } else {
                    patchItem(routes, i, { from: v.trim() }, onPatch, "routes");
                  }
                }}
              />
              <Field label="РћС‚РєСѓРґР°" value={String(it.from || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(routes, i, { from: v }, onPatch, "routes")} />
              <Field label="РљСѓРґР°" value={String(it.to || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(routes, i, { to: v }, onPatch, "routes")} />
              <Field label="Р’ РїСѓС‚Рё" value={String(it.duration || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(routes, i, { duration: v }, onPatch, "routes")} />
              <Field label="Р¦РµРЅР° РѕС‚" value={String(it.minPrice ?? "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(routes, i, { minPrice: toNumberOrZero(v) }, onPatch, "routes")} />
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Checkbox
                  checked={it.isPopular === true}

              <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ С†РµРЅС‹" value={String(it.priceColor || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(routes, i, { priceColor: v }, onPatch, "routes")} />

              <Field label="Р’РѕР·СЂР°СЃС‚" value={it.age || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { age: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="Р¦РµРЅР°" value={it.price || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { price: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РђСЂС‚РёСЃС‚ / СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ" value={it.artist || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { artist: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="Р”Р°С‚Р°" value={it.date || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { date: v }, onPatch)} />

              <Field label="Р“СЂР°РґРёРµРЅС‚ (tailwind, Р·Р°РїР°СЃРЅРѕР№)" value={it.color || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { color: v }, onPatch)} />
              <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°СЃС‚Р°РІРєРё" value={it.bgColor || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { bgColor: v }, onPatch)} />
              <MediaField label="Р¤РѕС‚Рѕ Р·Р°СЃС‚Р°РІРєРё" value={it.image || ""} accept="image/*" onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { image: v }, onPatch)} />
              <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°" value={it.textColor || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { textColor: v }, onPatch)} />
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (sectionId === "inspiration") {

        <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={str("titleColor")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ titleColor: v })} />
        <Field label="РџРѕРґР·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("subtitle")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ subtitle: v })} multiline />

        {items.map((raw, i) => {
          const it = raw as Record<string, string>;
          return (

              <Field label="РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ" value={it.description || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { description: v }, onPatch)} multiline />
              <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёСЏ" value={it.descriptionColor || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { descriptionColor: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РЎСЃС‹Р»РєР°" value={it.href || ""} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { href: v }, onPatch)} />
              <MediaField label="Р¤РѕС‚Рѕ" value={it.image || ""} accept="image/*" onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { image: v }, onPatch)} />
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (sectionId === "testimonials") {
    const items = Array.isArray(fields.items) ? fields.items : [];
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Field label="Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("title")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ title: v })} />
        <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={str("titleColor")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ titleColor: v })} />
        <Field label="РџРѕРґР·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє" value={str("subtitle")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ subtitle: v })} multiline />
        <CmsColorField label="Р¦РІРµС‚ РїРѕРґР·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°" value={str("subtitleColor")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ subtitleColor: v })} />
        {items.map((raw, i) => {
          const it = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
          const gallery = Array.isArray(it.gallery) ? (it.gallery as string[]) : [];
          return (
            <div key={i} className="space-y-2 rounded-md border p-3">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold">{String(it.name || `РћС‚Р·С‹РІ ${i + 1}`)}</p>
              <Field label="ID" value={String(it.id ?? "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { id: Number(v) || v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РРјСЏ" value={String(it.name || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { name: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РўРµРєСЃС‚ РЅР° РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРµ" value={String(it.text || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { text: v }, onPatch)} multiline />
              <Field label="РњР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚" value={String(it.route || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { route: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="Р”Р°С‚Р°" value={String(it.date || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { date: v }, onPatch)} />
              <MediaField label="РђРІР°С‚Р°СЂ" value={String(it.avatar || "")} accept="image/*" onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { avatar: v }, onPatch)} />
              <MediaField label="Р¤РѕС‚Рѕ РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРё" value={String(it.photo || "")} accept="image/*" onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { photo: v }, onPatch)} />
              <Field label="РўРµРєСЃС‚ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РѕС‚Р·С‹РІР°" value={String(it.body || "")} onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { body: v }, onPatch)} multiline />
              <MediaField
                label="Р”РѕРї. С„РѕС‚Рѕ РІ РѕС‚Р·С‹РІРµ (РґРѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ)"
                value=""
                accept="image/*"
                onChange={(v) => patchItem(items, i, { gallery: [...gallery, v] }, onPatch)}
              />
              {gallery.length > 0 && (
                <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {gallery.map((g, gi) => (
                    <li key={gi} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="truncate flex-1">{g}</span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="text-destructive"
                        onClick={() =>
                          patchItem(
                            items,
                            i,
                            { gallery: gallery.filter((_, x) => x !== gi) },
                            onPatch
                          )
                        }
                      >
                        СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ
                      </button>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (sectionId === "blogInvite") {
    const articleIds = Array.isArray(fields.articleIds)
      ? (fields.articleIds as string[])
      : [];
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Field label="Р‘РµР№РґР¶" value={str("badge")} onChange={(v) => onPatch({ badge: v })} />

}

const getBusStation = (city: string, index: number = 0): string => {
  const stations = busStationMap[city] || [city];
  return stations[index % stations.length];
};

const BusSearch = () => {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const initialFromCity = searchParams.get("from") || "РњРѕСЃРєРІР°";
  const initialToCity = searchParams.get("to") || "РЎР°РЅРєС‚-РџРµС‚РµСЂР±СѓСЂРі";
  const dateStr = searchParams.get("date");
  const initialPassengers = searchParams.get("passengers") || "1";

  const [fromCity, setFromCity] = useState(initialFromCity);
  const [toCity, setToCity] = useState(initialToCity);
  const [passengers, setPassengers] = useState(initialPassengers);
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState<Date>(dateStr ? new Date(dateStr) : new Date());

  const departureDate = selectedDate;

  const routes = generateRoutes(fromCity, toCity, departureDate);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [fromCity, toCity, selectedDate]);

  // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј URL РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРІ
  const updateSearchParams = (newFrom?: string, newTo?: string, newDate?: Date, newPassengers?: string) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set("from", newFrom || fromCity);
    params.set("to", newTo || toCity);
    params.set("date", format(newDate || selectedDate, "yyyy-MM-dd"));
    params.set("passengers", newPassengers || passengers);
    setSearchParams(params);
  };

  const handleSwapCities = () => {
    const newFrom = toCity;
    const newTo = fromCity;
    setFromCity(newFrom);
    setToCity(newTo);
    updateSearchParams(newFrom, newTo);
  };

  const handleDateChange = (date: Date) => {
    setSelectedDate(date);
    updateSearchParams(undefined, undefined, date);
  };

  const handleSearch = () => {
    updateSearchParams();
  };

  // Р“РµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј РґР°С‚С‹ РґР»СЏ РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЏ (РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅР°СЏ + 2 РґРѕ/РїРѕСЃР»Рµ)
  const getDateRange = () => {
    const dates: Date[] = [];
    for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {
      dates.push(addDays(selectedDate, i));
    }
    return dates;
  };

  const dateRange = getDateRange();

  const formatDate = (date: Date) => {
    return format(date, "d MMM, EEE", { locale: ru });
  };

  const formatShortDate = (date: Date) => {
    const day = format(date, "d", { locale: ru });
    const month = format(date, "MMM", { locale: ru });
    const weekday = format(date, "EEE", { locale: ru });
    // РЎРѕРєСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ РґРЅРµР№ РЅРµРґРµР»Рё РґРѕ 3 Р±СѓРєРІ
    const dayMap: Record<string, string> = {
      "РїРЅ": "РїРЅРґ",
      "РІС‚": "РІС‚СЂ", 
      "СЃСЂ": "СЃСЂРґ",
      "С‡С‚": "С‡С‚РІ",
      "РїС‚": "РїС‚РЅ",
      "СЃР±": "СЃР±С‚",
      "РІСЃ": "РІСЃРє"
    };
    const shortWeekday = dayMap[weekday] || weekday;
    return `${day} ${month}, ${shortWeekday}`;
  };

  const formatReviewCount = (count: number) => {
    if (count >= 1000) {
      return `${(count / 1000).toFixed(1)}Рљ`;
    }
    return count.toString();
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
      <Header />
      <main className="container pt-24 pb-8 md:pt-32">
        {/* Р¤РѕСЂРјР° РїРѕРёСЃРєР° */}
        <Card className="mb-6">
          <CardContent className="p-6">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch">
                {/* РћС‚РєСѓРґР° */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РћС‚РєСѓРґР°</label>
                  <Select value={fromCity} onValueChange={(value) => {
                    setFromCity(value);
                    updateSearchParams(value, undefined);
                  }}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-12">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      {cities.map((city) => (
                        <SelectItem key={`from-${city}`} value={city}>
                          {city}
                        </SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                {/* РљРЅРѕРїРєР° РѕР±РјРµРЅР° */}
                <div className="flex items-end">
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="icon"
                    className="h-12 w-12"
                    onClick={handleSwapCities}
                  >
                    <ArrowLeftRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </Button>
                </div>

                {/* РљСѓРґР° */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РљСѓРґР°</label>
                  <Select value={toCity} onValueChange={(value) => {
                    setToCity(value);
                    updateSearchParams(undefined, value);
                  }}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-12">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      {cities.map((city) => (
                        <SelectItem key={`to-${city}`} value={city}>
                          {city}
                        </SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                {/* РљРѕРіРґР° */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РљРѕРіРґР°</label>
                  <Popover>
                    <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                      <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        className={cn(
                          "h-12 w-full justify-start text-left font-normal px-3",
                          "text-sm"
                        )}
                      >
                        <CalendarIcon className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 text-muted-foreground" />
                        {formatDate(selectedDate)}
                      </Button>
                    </PopoverTrigger>
                    <PopoverContent className="w-auto p-0" align="start">
                      <Calendar
                        mode="single"
                        selected={selectedDate}
                        onSelect={(date) => {
                          if (date) {
                            handleDateChange(date);
                          }
                        }}
                        initialFocus
                        numberOfMonths={2}
                        disabled={(date) => date < new Date()}
                        className="pointer-events-auto"
                      />
                    </PopoverContent>
                  </Popover>
                </div>

                {/* РљС‚Рѕ РµРґРµС‚ */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РљС‚Рѕ РµРґРµС‚</label>
                  <Select value={passengers} onValueChange={(value) => {
                    setPassengers(value);
                    updateSearchParams(undefined, undefined, undefined, value);
                  }}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-12">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="1">1 РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂ</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="2">2 РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="3">3 РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="4">4 РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                {/* РљРЅРѕРїРєР° РїРѕРёСЃРєР° */}
                <div className="flex items-end">
                  <Button
                    className="h-12 px-8 bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700"
                    onClick={handleSearch}
                  >
                    РќР°Р№С‚Рё Р°РІС‚РѕР±СѓСЃС‹
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* РљР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЊ СЃ РґР°С‚Р°РјРё */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-4">
                <Popover>
                  <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                    <button className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm flex-shrink-0 border border-purple-200 hover:bg-purple-50 transition-colors">
                      <CalendarIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-600" />
                    </button>
                  </PopoverTrigger>
                  <PopoverContent className="w-auto p-0" align="start">
                    <Calendar
                      mode="single"
                      selected={selectedDate}
                      onSelect={(date) => {
                        if (date) {
                          handleDateChange(date);
                        }
                      }}
                      initialFocus
                      numberOfMonths={2}
                      disabled={(date) => date < new Date()}
                      className="pointer-events-auto"

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={index}

                        )}
                      >
                        <div className="font-semibold">{formatShortDate(date)}</div>
                        <div className={cn(
                          "text-xs mt-1",
                          isSelected ? "text-white/90" : "text-muted-foreground"

            </p>

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import Header from "@/components/Header";
import Footer from "@/components/Footer";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";

import { Clock, Loader2, Bell } from "lucide-react";

import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import PassengerForm, { PassengerData } from "@/components/checkout/PassengerForm";

import BuyerForm, { BuyerData } from "@/components/checkout/BuyerForm";

const Checkout = () => {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [showAdditionalServices, setShowAdditionalServices] = useState(false);
  const [userProfile, setUserProfile] = useState<any>(null);
  const [selectPassengerModalOpen, setSelectPassengerModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [currentPassengerIndex, setCurrentPassengerIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [consentChecked, setConsentChecked] = useState(false);

  const fromCity = searchParams.get("from") || "РњРѕСЃРєРІР°";
  const toCity = searchParams.get("to") || "РЎР°РЅРєС‚-РџРµС‚РµСЂР±СѓСЂРі";
  const dateStr = searchParams.get("date");
  const carNumber = searchParams.get("carNumber") || "";
  const seatNumber = searchParams.get("seatNumber") || "";
  const seatType = searchParams.get("seatType") || "";
  const price = searchParams.get("price") || "0";
  const adults = parseInt(searchParams.get("adults") || "1");
  const children = parseInt(searchParams.get("children") || "0");
  const infants = parseInt(searchParams.get("infants") || "0");

  const departureDate = dateStr ? new Date(dateStr) : new Date();

  // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°, Р±С‹Р» Р»Рё РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ РјРµСЃС‚Р°
  const hasSelectedSeat = carNumber && seatNumber;

  // РРЅРёС†РёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ
  const totalPassengers = adults + children + infants;
  
  // Р—Р°С‰РёС‚Р° РѕС‚ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°СЏ, РєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµС‚ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ - РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј useMemo РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°Р±РёР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё
  const safeAdults = useMemo(() => Math.max(1, adults), [adults]);
  const safeChildren = useMemo(() => Math.max(0, children), [children]);
  const safeInfants = useMemo(() => Math.max(0, infants), [infants]);
  const safeTotalPassengers = useMemo(() => safeAdults + safeChildren + safeInfants, [safeAdults, safeChildren, safeInfants]);
  
  const createPassengerData = (adultsCount: number, childrenCount: number, infantsCount: number): PassengerData[] => {
    const initial: PassengerData[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < adultsCount; i++) {
      initial.push({
        surname: "",
        name: "",
        patronymic: "",
        noPatronymic: false,
        gender: "",
        birthDate: "",
        documentType: "",
        documentSeries: "",
        documentNumber: "",
      });
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < childrenCount; i++) {
      initial.push({
        surname: "",
        name: "",
        patronymic: "",
        noPatronymic: false,
        gender: "",
        birthDate: "",
        documentType: "",
        documentSeries: "",
        documentNumber: "",
      });
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < infantsCount; i++) {
      initial.push({
        surname: "",
        name: "",
        patronymic: "",
        noPatronymic: false,
        gender: "",
        birthDate: "",
        documentType: "",
        documentSeries: "",
        documentNumber: "",
      });
    }
    return initial;
  };
  
  const [passengers, setPassengers] = useState<PassengerData[]>(() => 
    createPassengerData(safeAdults, safeChildren, safeInfants)
  );
  
  // РЎРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РґР»СЏ РёРјРµРЅ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ
  const [passengerDisplayNames, setPassengerDisplayNames] = useState<string[]>(() => 
    Array(safeAdults + safeChildren + safeInfants).fill("")
  );
  
  // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРІ URL
  useEffect(() => {
    const expectedLength = safeAdults + safeChildren + safeInfants;
    setPassengers((prevPassengers) => {
      // РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ, РµСЃР»Рё РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ РЅРµ РёР·РјРµРЅРёР»РѕСЃСЊ
      if (prevPassengers.length === expectedLength) {
        // РљРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ РЅРµ РёР·РјРµРЅРёР»РѕСЃСЊ, СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ
        return prevPassengers;
      }
      // РљРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ РёР·РјРµРЅРёР»РѕСЃСЊ, РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ
      return createPassengerData(safeAdults, safeChildren, safeInfants);
    });
    // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ РёРјРµРЅ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІР°
    setPassengerDisplayNames((prevNames) => {
      if (prevNames.length === expectedLength) {
        return prevNames;
      }
      return Array(expectedLength).fill("");
    });
    // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ РѕС€РёР±РѕРє РґСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С‚РѕРІ РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІР°
    setDuplicateErrors((prevErrors) => {
      if (prevErrors.length === expectedLength) {
        return prevErrors;
      }
      return Array(expectedLength).fill("");
    });
    // РЎР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°РµРј РѕС€РёР±РєРё РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІР° РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ
    setErrors((prevErrors) => {
      if (prevErrors.passengers.length === expectedLength) {
        return prevErrors;
      }
      return {
        passengers: Array(expectedLength).fill(null).map(() => ({})),
        buyer: prevErrors.buyer,
      };
    });
  }, [safeAdults, safeChildren, safeInfants]);

  const [buyer, setBuyer] = useState<BuyerData>({
    surname: "",
    name: "",
    email: "",
    phone: "",
  });

  const [additionalServices, setAdditionalServices] = useState<AdditionalServicesData>({
    insurance: "",
    refund: "",
  });

  // РЎРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РґР»СЏ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚Р°
  const [certificateCode, setCertificateCode] = useState("");
  const [selectedCertificate, setSelectedCertificate] = useState<{
    id: string;
    certificate_code: string;
    amount: number;
    transport_type: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{
    passengers: Partial<Record<keyof PassengerData, string>>[];
    buyer: Partial<Record<keyof BuyerData, string>>;
  }>({
    passengers: [],
    buyer: {},
  });
  
  // РћС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅРѕРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РґР»СЏ РѕС€РёР±РѕРє РґСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С‚РѕРІ
  const [duplicateErrors, setDuplicateErrors] = useState<string[]>(() => 
    Array(safeAdults + safeChildren + safeInfants).fill("")
  );

  const [hasShownAuthWarning, setHasShownAuthWarning] = useState(false);

  const [hasLoadedProfile, setHasLoadedProfile] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    
    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёР·Р°С†РёРё РїСЂРё РІС‹Р±РѕСЂРµ РјРµСЃС‚Р° (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ СЂР°Р·)
    if (hasSelectedSeat && !user && !hasShownAuthWarning) {
      setHasShownAuthWarning(true);
      toast({
        title: "РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ",
        description: "Р”Р»СЏ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂР° РјРµСЃС‚Р° РЅРµРѕР±С…РѕРґРёРјРѕ СЃРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° Р·Р°СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }

    // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° РїСЂРѕС„РёР»СЏ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ СЂР°Р· РґР»СЏ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ)
    if (user?.id && !hasLoadedProfile) {
      setHasLoadedProfile(true);
      loadUserProfile();
    } else if (!user?.id) {
      setHasLoadedProfile(false);
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [user?.id, hasSelectedSeat]);

  const loadUserProfile = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    try {
      const data = await apiFetch<Record<string, unknown> | null>(
        "/api/user-profiles/me"
      );
      
      // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РїСЂРѕС„РёР»СЊ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РёР·РјРµРЅРёР»РёСЃСЊ
      setUserProfile((prevProfile) => {
        if (!data) return null;
        if (prevProfile?.id === data?.id) {
          return prevProfile;
        }
        return data as typeof prevProfile;
      });

      // РђРІС‚РѕР·Р°РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёРµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РїРѕРєСѓРїР°С‚РµР»СЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РѕРЅРё РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ
      if (data) {
        setBuyer((prevBuyer) => {
          // Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РІСЃРµ РїРѕР»СЏ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ
          const isEmpty = !prevBuyer.surname && !prevBuyer.name && !prevBuyer.email && !prevBuyer.phone;
          if (!isEmpty) {
            return prevBuyer;
          }
          return {
            surname: (data.last_name || "").toUpperCase(),
            name: (data.first_name || "").toUpperCase(),
            email: data.email || user.email || "",
            phone: data.phone || "",
          };
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error loading user profile:", error);
    }
  };

  const handlePassengerChange = (index: number, data: PassengerData) => {
    const newPassengers = [...passengers];
    newPassengers[index] = data;
    setPassengers(newPassengers);
    
    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РЅР° РґСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С‚С‹
    const duplicateIndex = newPassengers.findIndex((p, i) => 
      i !== index &&
      p.surname.trim() &&
      p.name.trim() &&
      p.documentSeries.trim() &&
      p.documentNumber.trim() &&
      p.surname.trim() === data.surname.trim() &&
      p.name.trim() === data.name.trim() &&
      p.documentSeries.trim() === data.documentSeries.trim() &&
      p.documentNumber.trim() === data.documentNumber.trim()
    );
    
    // РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј РѕС€РёР±РєРё РґР»СЏ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°
    const newErrors = [...errors.passengers];
    const newDuplicateErrors = [...duplicateErrors];
    
    if (duplicateIndex !== -1) {
      // РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅР°РІР»РёРІР°РµРј РѕС€РёР±РєСѓ РґСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С‚Р°
      newDuplicateErrors[index] = "Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР° СѓР¶Рµ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ РІ РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРµ";
      newDuplicateErrors[duplicateIndex] = "Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР° СѓР¶Рµ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ РІ РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРµ";
      // РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Рµ РѕС€РёР±РєРё РґР»СЏ СЌС‚РёС… РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ
      newErrors[index] = {};
      newErrors[duplicateIndex] = {};
    } else {
      // РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј РѕС€РёР±РєСѓ РґСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С‚Р°
      newDuplicateErrors[index] = "";
      // РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј РѕС€РёР±РєСѓ РґСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С‚Р° Сѓ РґСЂСѓРіРёС… РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ, РµСЃР»Рё РѕРЅР° Р±С‹Р»Р°
      newDuplicateErrors.forEach((err, i) => {
        if (i !== index && err === "Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР° СѓР¶Рµ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ РІ РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРµ") {
          newDuplicateErrors[i] = "";
        }
      });
      newErrors[index] = {};
    }
    setErrors({ ...errors, passengers: newErrors });
    setDuplicateErrors(newDuplicateErrors);
  };

  const handleBuyerChange = (data: BuyerData) => {
    setBuyer(data);
    // РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј РѕС€РёР±РєРё РїРѕРєСѓРїР°С‚РµР»СЏ
    setErrors({ ...errors, buyer: {} });
  };

  const validatePassenger = (passenger: PassengerData, index: number): Partial<Record<keyof PassengerData, string>> => {
    const passengerErrors: Partial<Record<keyof PassengerData, string>> = {};
    
    if (!passenger.surname.trim()) {
      passengerErrors.surname = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!passenger.name.trim()) {
      passengerErrors.name = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!passenger.noPatronymic && !passenger.patronymic.trim()) {
      passengerErrors.patronymic = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!passenger.gender) {
      passengerErrors.gender = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!passenger.birthDate) {
      passengerErrors.birthDate = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!passenger.documentType) {
      passengerErrors.documentType = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!passenger.documentSeries.trim() || !passenger.documentNumber.trim()) {
      passengerErrors.documentSeries = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }

    return passengerErrors;
  };

  const validateBuyer = (): Partial<Record<keyof BuyerData, string>> => {
    const buyerErrors: Partial<Record<keyof BuyerData, string>> = {};
    
    if (!buyer.surname.trim()) {
      buyerErrors.surname = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!buyer.name.trim()) {
      buyerErrors.name = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!buyer.email.trim()) {
      buyerErrors.email = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    } else if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(buyer.email)) {
      buyerErrors.email = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }
    if (!buyer.phone.trim()) {
      buyerErrors.phone = "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ";
    }

    return buyerErrors;
  };

  // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё (Р±РµР· setState)
  const checkFormValid = (): boolean => {
    const passengerErrors = passengers.map((p, i) => validatePassenger(p, i));
    const buyerErrors = validateBuyer();

    const hasPassengerErrors = passengerErrors.some(e => Object.keys(e).length > 0);
    const hasBuyerErrors = Object.keys(buyerErrors).length > 0;

    return !hasPassengerErrors && !hasBuyerErrors;
  };

  // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ РІР°Р»РёРґР°С†РёРё СЃ СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєРѕР№ РѕС€РёР±РѕРє (РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСЂРё submit)
  const validateAll = (): boolean => {
    const passengerErrors = passengers.map((p, i) => validatePassenger(p, i));
    const buyerErrors = validateBuyer();

    setErrors({
      passengers: passengerErrors,
      buyer: buyerErrors,
    });

    const hasPassengerErrors = passengerErrors.some(e => Object.keys(e).length > 0);
    const hasBuyerErrors = Object.keys(buyerErrors).length > 0;

    return !hasPassengerErrors && !hasBuyerErrors;
  };

  const handleContinue = () => {
    if (!validateAll()) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР° РІР°Р»РёРґР°С†РёРё",
        description: "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РІСЃРµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!consentChecked) {
      toast({
        title: "РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ СЃРѕРіР»Р°СЃРёРµ",
        description: "РќРµРѕР±С…РѕРґРёРјРѕ РґР°С‚СЊ СЃРѕРіР»Р°СЃРёРµ РЅР° РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєСѓ РїРµСЂСЃРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    setShowAdditionalServices(true);
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };

  const handlePay = async () => {
    if (!validateAll()) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР° РІР°Р»РёРґР°С†РёРё",
        description: "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РІСЃРµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!additionalServices.insurance || !additionalServices.refund) {
      toast({
        title: "Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РѕРїС†РёРё",
        description: "РќРµРѕР±С…РѕРґРёРјРѕ РІС‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ РѕРїС†РёСЋ РІ РєР°Р¶РґРѕР№ РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРµ РґРѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹С… СѓСЃР»СѓРі",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!user) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР° Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёР·Р°С†РёРё",
        description: "РќРµРѕР±С…РѕРґРёРјРѕ РІРѕР№С‚Рё РІ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    setIsProcessing(true);

    try {
      if (passengers.length === 0) {
        throw new Error("РќРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ");
      }

      // РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј С‚РёРї С‚СЂР°РЅСЃРїРѕСЂС‚Р° РёР· URL РёР»Рё РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј train РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ
      const transportType = searchParams.get("travelType") || "train";

      // Р“РµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј Р±Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ Р·Р°РєР°Р·Р°
      const baseOrderNumber = `T${Date.now().toString().slice(-10)}`;

      // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј РІСЂРµРјСЏ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ Рё РїСЂРёР±С‹С‚РёСЏ (РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј fallback Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ)
      const depTime = "00:25"; // РњРѕР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ РёР· routeId, РЅРѕ РїРѕРєР° РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј fallback
      const arrTime = "09:26"; // РњРѕР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ РёР· routeId, РЅРѕ РїРѕРєР° РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј fallback
      
      // Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚РёСЂСѓРµРј РґР°С‚С‹
      const depDateFormatted = format(departureDate, "dd.MM.yyyy", { locale: ru });
      const arrDateFormatted = format(departureDate, "dd.MM.yyyy", { locale: ru });

      // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ СЃС‚Р°РЅС†РёР№ (РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј fallback)
      const depStation = getStation(fromCity, 0);
      const arrStation = getStation(toCity, 0);
      const depAddress = `Рі. ${fromCity}, РІРѕРєР·Р°Р»`;
      const arrAddress = `Рі. ${toCity}, РІРѕРєР·Р°Р»`;

      // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅРёСЏ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° С‚РёРїР° РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°
      const getDocumentTypeText = (docType: string): string => {
        switch (docType) {
          case "passport":
            return "РїР°СЃРїРѕСЂС‚";
          case "birth_certificate":
            return "СЃРІРёРґРµС‚РµР»СЊСЃС‚РІРѕ Рѕ СЂРѕР¶РґРµРЅРёРё";
          case "foreign_passport":
            return "Р·Р°РіСЂР°РЅРїР°СЃРїРѕСЂС‚";
          case "foreign_document":
            return "РёРЅРѕСЃС‚СЂР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚";
          default:
            return "РїР°СЃРїРѕСЂС‚";
        }
      };

      // РњР°СЃСЃРёРІ РґР»СЏ С…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РІСЃРµС… Р±РёР»РµС‚РѕРІ
      const ticketsData: EpdData[] = [];

      // Р“РµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ Р±РёР»РµС‚РѕРІ РґР»СЏ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР° (Р±РµР· РіРµРЅРµСЂР°С†РёРё PDF)
      for (let i = 0; i < passengers.length; i++) {
        const passenger = passengers[i];
        
        // Р“РµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂ Р·Р°РєР°Р·Р° РґР»СЏ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°
        const orderNumber = `${baseOrderNumber}${i}`;
        
        // Р¤РѕСЂРјРёСЂСѓРµРј РїРѕР»РЅРѕРµ РёРјСЏ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°
        const passengerFullName = `${passenger.surname} ${passenger.name} ${passenger.patronymic || ""}`.trim();
        
        // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј С‚РёРї РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°
        const documentTypeDisplay = getDocumentTypeText(passenger.documentType);

        // Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚РёСЂСѓРµРј РґР°С‚Сѓ СЂРѕР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ (Р±РµР· Р Р¤)
        const birthDateFormatted = passenger.birthDate 
          ? format(new Date(passenger.birthDate), "dd.MM.yyyy", { locale: ru })
          : "";

        // РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РґР»СЏ Р±РёР»РµС‚Р°
        const epd: EpdData = {
          orderNumber,
          trainNumber: "022Рђ", // Fallback, РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ РёР· routeId
          trainName: "РќРѕС‡РЅРѕР№ СЌРєСЃРїСЂРµСЃСЃ", // Fallback, РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ РёР· routeId
          carriage: carNumber || "1",
          seat: seatNumber || "1",
          seatType: seatType === "upper" ? "Р’РµСЂС…РЅРµРµ" : seatType === "lower" ? "РќРёР¶РЅРµРµ" : "РњРµСЃС‚Рѕ",
          depTime,
          depDate: depDateFormatted,
          depCity: fromCity,
          depStation,
          depAddress,
          depTz: "+3",
          arrTime,
          arrDate: arrDateFormatted,
          arrCity: toCity,
          arrStation,
          arrAddress,
          arrTz: "+3",
          passengerFullName,
          documentType: documentTypeDisplay,
          documentSeries: passenger.documentSeries || "",
          documentNumber: passenger.documentNumber || "",
          birthDate: birthDateFormatted,
          routeShort: `${fromCity} вЂ” ${toCity}`,
          infoText: "Р’С‹ Р·Р°СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅС‹ РЅР° СЂРµР№СЃ. Р‘РёР»РµС‚ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ СЂР°СЃРїРµС‡Р°С‚Р°С‚СЊ РёР»Рё РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ РЅР° СЌРєСЂР°РЅРµ РјРѕР±РёР»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ СѓСЃС‚СЂРѕР№СЃС‚РІР°. РџСЂРё РїРѕСЃР°РґРєРµ РїСЂРµРґСЉСЏРІРёС‚Рµ РїСЂРѕРІРѕРґРЅРёРєСѓ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚, СѓРґРѕСЃС‚РѕРІРµСЂСЏСЋС‰РёР№ Р»РёС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ.",
        };

        // РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ Р±РёР»РµС‚Р°
        ticketsData.push(epd);
      }

      // РЎРѕР·РґР°С‘Рј Р·Р°РєР°Р· РІ Р‘Р” Р”Рћ РѕРїР»Р°С‚С‹
      const insertedTicket = await apiFetch<{ id: string }>("/api/tickets", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          order_number: baseOrderNumber,
          transport_type: transportType,
          total_price: totalPrice,
          tickets_data: ticketsData,
          from_city: fromCity,
          to_city: toCity,
          departure_date: format(departureDate, "yyyy-MM-dd"),
          electronic_registration_status: "pending",
          payment_status: "pending",
        }),
      });

      if (!insertedTicket?.id) {
        throw new Error("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРё Р·Р°РєР°Р·Р°");
      }

      // РџРѕРјРµС‡Р°РµРј СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚ РєР°Рє РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№, РµСЃР»Рё РѕРЅ Р±С‹Р» РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРµРЅ
      if (selectedCertificate && certificateCode) {
        try {
          await apiFetch(`/api/certificates/${selectedCertificate.id}`, {
            method: "PATCH",
            body: JSON.stringify({
              status: "used",
              used_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            }),
          });
        } catch (certError) {
          console.error("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРё СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚Р°:", certError);
        }
      }

      const payResult = await apiFetch<{
        skip_webpay?: boolean;
        order_number?: string;
        url?: string;
        payment_id?: string;
      }>("/api/webpay-create", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ticket_id: insertedTicket.id,
          order_number: baseOrderNumber,
          total_price: totalPrice,
          base_url: window.location.origin,
        }),
      });

      if (payResult.skip_webpay && payResult.order_number) {
        setIsProcessing(false);
        navigate(
          `/payment/success?order=${encodeURIComponent(payResult.order_number)}`
        );
        return;
      }

      if (payResult.url) {
        window.location.href = payResult.url;
        return;
      }
      throw new Error("РќРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅС‹Р№ РѕС‚РІРµС‚ РѕРїР»Р°С‚С‹ (РѕР¶РёРґР°Р»СЃСЏ URL Р®Kassa)");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРё РїР»Р°С‚РµР¶Р°:", error);
      setIsProcessing(false);
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СЃРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ РїР»Р°С‚РµР¶. РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№С‚Рµ РµС‰С‘ СЂР°Р·.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
  };

  const formatDate = (date: Date) => {
    return format(date, "d MMM, EEE", { locale: ru });
  };

  const basePrice = parseInt(price) * adults + (parseInt(price) * 0.7 * children);
  
  // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ РґРѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹С… СѓСЃР»СѓРі
  const additionalServicesPrice = 
    (additionalServices.insurance === "yes" ? 299 : 0) +
    (additionalServices.refund === "yes" ? 550 : 0);
  
  // РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј СЃРєРёРґРєСѓ РїРѕ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚Сѓ
  const certificateDiscount = selectedCertificate?.amount || 0;
  
  const totalPrice = Math.max(0, basePrice + additionalServicesPrice - certificateDiscount);

  const isFormValid = () => {
    return checkFormValid() && consentChecked;
  };

  const getPassengerType = (index: number): "adult" | "child" | "infant" => {
    if (index < safeAdults) return "adult";
    if (index < safeAdults + safeChildren) return "child";
    return "infant";
  };

  const handleSelectPassenger = (index: number) => {
    if (!user) {
      toast({
        title: "РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ",
        description: "Р”Р»СЏ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР° РЅРµРѕР±С…РѕРґРёРјРѕ РІРѕР№С‚Рё РІ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }
    setCurrentPassengerIndex(index);
    setSelectPassengerModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handlePassengerSelected = (passengerData: PassengerData, displayName?: string) => {
    if (currentPassengerIndex !== null) {
      handlePassengerChange(currentPassengerIndex, passengerData);
      // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РёРјСЏ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ
      const newDisplayNames = [...passengerDisplayNames];
      newDisplayNames[currentPassengerIndex] = displayName || "";
      setPassengerDisplayNames(newDisplayNames);
    }
    setSelectPassengerModalOpen(false);
    setCurrentPassengerIndex(null);
  };

  const handleUseAccountData = () => {
    if (userProfile) {
      setBuyer({
        surname: (userProfile.last_name || "").toUpperCase(),
        name: (userProfile.first_name || "").toUpperCase(),
        email: userProfile.email || user?.email || "",
        phone: userProfile.phone || "",
      });
    }
  };

  if (showAdditionalServices) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
        <Header />
        <main className="container pt-24 pb-8 md:pt-32">
          <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">
            <h1 className="heading-gradient text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-8">РћС„РѕСЂРјР»РµРЅРёРµ Р·Р°РєР°Р·Р°</h1>

            {/* РРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ Рѕ РїРѕРµР·РґРєРµ (РєРѕРјРїР°РєС‚РЅР°СЏ) */}
            <Card className="mb-6">
              <CardContent className="p-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <Bell className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-600" />
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-medium">{fromCity} вЂ” {toCity}</div>
                      <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                        {formatDate(departureDate)} вЂў {safeTotalPassengers} {safeTotalPassengers === 1 ? "РїР°СЃСЃ" : "РїР°СЃСЃ"}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>

            {/* Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ СѓСЃР»СѓРіРё */}
            <AdditionalServices
              data={additionalServices}
              onChange={setAdditionalServices}
              totalPassengers={safeTotalPassengers}
            />

            {/* РЎС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ Рё РєРЅРѕРїРєР° РѕРїР»Р°С‚С‹ */}
            <Card className="mb-6">
              <CardContent className="p-6">
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {/* РџРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР° СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚Р° */}
                  {user && (
                    <CertificateInput
                      value={certificateCode}
                      onChange={(code) => {
                        setCertificateCode(code);
                        // Р•СЃР»Рё РєРѕРґ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ СЃРїРёСЃРєРµ, РѕС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚
                        if (!code || code.length !== 10) {
                          setSelectedCertificate(null);
                        }
                      }}
                      transportType={searchParams.get("travelType") || "train"}
                      onCertificateSelect={(cert) => {
                        setSelectedCertificate(cert);
                        if (cert) {
                          setCertificateCode(cert.certificate_code);
                        }
                      }}
                      userId={user.id}
                    />
                  )}

                  {/* РћС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ СЃРєРёРґРєРё РїРѕ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚Сѓ */}
                  {certificateDiscount > 0 && (
                    <div className="bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 border border-green-200 dark:border-green-800 rounded-lg p-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                        <span className="text-green-800 dark:text-green-200">
                          РЎРєРёРґРєР° РїРѕ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚Сѓ:
                        </span>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-green-700 dark:text-green-300">
                          -{certificateDiscount.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} в‚Ѕ
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="text-center">
                    <div className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2">
                      {totalPrice.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} в‚Ѕ
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      Р·Р° {safeTotalPassengers} {safeTotalPassengers === 1 ? "РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°" : "РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ"}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <Checkbox
                      id="consent"
                      checked={consentChecked}
                      onCheckedChange={(checked) => setConsentChecked(checked === true)}
                    />
                    <Label htmlFor="consent" className="text-sm cursor-pointer">
                      Р”Р°СЋ СЃРѕРіР»Р°СЃРёРµ РЅР° РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєСѓ РїРµСЂСЃРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…
                    </Label>
                  </div>

                  <Button
                    className={cn(
                      "w-full text-lg py-6",
                      isProcessing || !additionalServices.insurance || !additionalServices.refund || !consentChecked
                        ? "bg-purple-300 hover:bg-purple-300 cursor-not-allowed opacity-60"
                        : "bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700"
                    )}
                    onClick={handlePay}
                    disabled={isProcessing || !additionalServices.insurance || !additionalServices.refund || !consentChecked}
                  >
                    {isProcessing ? (
                      <>
                        <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                        РћР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєР°...
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      `РџСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РёС‚СЊ`
                    )}
                  </Button>

                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground text-center">
                    РќР°Р¶РёРјР°СЏ В«РџРµСЂРµР№С‚Рё Рє РѕРїР»Р°С‚РµВ», РІС‹ СЃРѕРіР»Р°С€Р°РµС‚РµСЃСЊ СЃ РїСѓР±Р»РёС‡РЅРѕР№ РѕС„РµСЂС‚РѕР№. Р’ С†РµРЅСѓ
                    РІРєР»СЋС‡С‘РЅ СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃРЅС‹Р№ СЃР±РѕСЂ
                  </p>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </div>
        </main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Р—Р°С‰РёС‚Р° РѕС‚ РїСѓСЃС‚РѕРіРѕ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІР° РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ
  if (passengers.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
        <Header />
        <main className="container pt-24 pb-8 md:pt-32">
          <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">
            <h1 className="heading-gradient text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-8">РћС€РёР±РєР°</h1>
            <Card>
              <CardContent className="p-6">
                <p className="text-muted-foreground">
                  РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ Рѕ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°С…. РџРѕР¶Р°Р»СѓР№СЃС‚Р°, РІРµСЂРЅРёС‚РµСЃСЊ РЅР°Р·Р°Рґ Рё РїРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№С‚Рµ СЃРЅРѕРІР°.
                </p>
                <Button onClick={() => navigate(-1)} className="mt-4">
                  РќР°Р·Р°Рґ
                </Button>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </div>
        </main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
      <Header />
      <main className="container pt-24 pb-8 md:pt-32">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">
          <h1 className="heading-gradient text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-8">Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ</h1>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {/* Р›РµРІР°СЏ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєР° - Р¤РѕСЂРјС‹ */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
              {/* Р¤РѕСЂРјС‹ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ */}
              {passengers.map((passenger, index) => (
                <PassengerForm
                  key={index}
                  passengerNumber={index + 1}
                  passengerType={getPassengerType(index)}
                  data={passenger}
                  onChange={(data) => handlePassengerChange(index, data)}
                  errors={errors.passengers[index] || {}}
                  onSelectFromSaved={() => handleSelectPassenger(index)}
                  showSelectButton={!!user}
                  displayName={passengerDisplayNames[index]}
                  duplicateError={duplicateErrors[index]}
                />
              ))}

              {/* Р¤РѕСЂРјР° РїРѕРєСѓРїР°С‚РµР»СЏ */}
              <BuyerForm
                data={buyer}
                onChange={handleBuyerChange}
                errors={errors.buyer}
                userData={userProfile ? {
                  firstName: userProfile.first_name,
                  lastName: userProfile.last_name,
                  email: userProfile.email || user?.email,
                  phone: userProfile.phone,
                } : undefined}
                onUseAccountData={handleUseAccountData}
              />
            </div>

            {/* РџСЂР°РІР°СЏ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєР° - РРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ Рѕ РїРѕРµР·РґРєРµ Рё СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ */}
            <div className="space-y-6">
              {/* РРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ Рѕ РїРѕРµР·РґРєРµ */}
              <Card>
                <CardContent className="p-6">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
                    <Bell className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-600" />
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-medium">{fromCity} вЂ” {toCity}</div>
                      <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                        {formatDate(departureDate)} вЂў {safeTotalPassengers} {safeTotalPassengers === 1 ? "РїР°СЃСЃ" : "РїР°СЃСЃ"}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>

              {/* РЎС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ */}
              <Card>
                <CardContent className="p-6">
                  <div className="text-center mb-4">
                    <div className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2">
                      {totalPrice.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} в‚Ѕ
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      Р·Р° {safeTotalPassengers} {safeTotalPassengers === 1 ? "РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°" : "РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ"}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 mb-4">
                    <Checkbox
                      id="consent"
                      checked={consentChecked}
                      onCheckedChange={(checked) => setConsentChecked(checked === true)}
                    />
                    <Label htmlFor="consent" className="text-sm cursor-pointer">
                      Р”Р°СЋ СЃРѕРіР»Р°СЃРёРµ РЅР° РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєСѓ РїРµСЂСЃРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…
                    </Label>
                  </div>

                  <Button
                    className={cn(
                      "w-full text-lg py-6",
                      isFormValid()
                        ? "bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700"
                        : "bg-purple-300 hover:bg-purple-300 cursor-not-allowed opacity-60"
                    )}
                    onClick={handleContinue}
                    disabled={!isFormValid()}
                  >
                    РџРµСЂРµР№С‚Рё Рє РѕРїР»Р°С‚Рµ
                  </Button>

                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground text-center mt-4">
                    РќР°Р¶РёРјР°СЏ В«РџРµСЂРµР№С‚Рё Рє РѕРїР»Р°С‚РµВ», РІС‹ СЃРѕРіР»Р°С€Р°РµС‚РµСЃСЊ СЃ РїСѓР±Р»РёС‡РЅРѕР№ РѕС„РµСЂС‚РѕР№. Р’ С†РµРЅСѓ
                    РІРєР»СЋС‡С‘РЅ СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃРЅС‹Р№ СЃР±РѕСЂ
                  </p>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />

      {/* РњРѕРґР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ РѕРєРЅРѕ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂР° РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР° */}
      {user && (
        <SelectPassengerModal
          open={selectPassengerModalOpen}
          onClose={() => {
            setSelectPassengerModalOpen(false);
            setCurrentPassengerIndex(null);
          }}
          onSelect={handlePassengerSelected}
          userId={user.id}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default Checkout;

const FlightSearch = () => {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const initialFromCity = searchParams.get("from") || "РњРѕСЃРєРІР°";
  const initialToCity = searchParams.get("to") || "РЎР°РЅРєС‚-РџРµС‚РµСЂР±СѓСЂРі";
  const dateStr = searchParams.get("date");
  const initialPassengers = searchParams.get("passengers") || "1";
  const initialFlightClass = searchParams.get("class") || "economy";

  const [fromCity, setFromCity] = useState(initialFromCity);
  const [toCity, setToCity] = useState(initialToCity);
  const [passengers, setPassengers] = useState(initialPassengers);
  const [flightClass, setFlightClass] = useState(initialFlightClass);
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState<Date>(dateStr ? new Date(dateStr) : new Date());

  const departureDate = selectedDate;

  const routes = generateRoutes(fromCity, toCity, departureDate, flightClass);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [fromCity, toCity, selectedDate, flightClass]);

  // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј URL РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРІ
  const updateSearchParams = (newFrom?: string, newTo?: string, newDate?: Date, newPassengers?: string) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set("from", newFrom || fromCity);
    params.set("to", newTo || toCity);
    params.set("date", format(newDate || selectedDate, "yyyy-MM-dd"));
    params.set("passengers", newPassengers || passengers);
    params.set("class", flightClass);
    setSearchParams(params);
  };

  const handleSwapCities = () => {
    const newFrom = toCity;
    const newTo = fromCity;
    setFromCity(newFrom);
    setToCity(newTo);
    updateSearchParams(newFrom, newTo);
  };

  const handleDateChange = (date: Date) => {
    setSelectedDate(date);
    updateSearchParams(undefined, undefined, date);
  };

  const handleSearch = () => {
    updateSearchParams();
  };

  // Р“РµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј РґР°С‚С‹ РґР»СЏ РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЏ (РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅР°СЏ + 2 РґРѕ/РїРѕСЃР»Рµ)
  const getDateRange = () => {
    const dates: Date[] = [];
    for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {
      dates.push(addDays(selectedDate, i));
    }
    return dates;
  };

  const dateRange = getDateRange();

  const formatDate = (date: Date) => {
    return format(date, "d MMM, EEE", { locale: ru });
  };

  const formatShortDate = (date: Date) => {
    const day = format(date, "d", { locale: ru });
    const month = format(date, "MMM", { locale: ru });
    const weekday = format(date, "EEE", { locale: ru });
    // РЎРѕРєСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ РґРЅРµР№ РЅРµРґРµР»Рё РґРѕ 3 Р±СѓРєРІ
    const dayMap: Record<string, string> = {
      "РїРЅ": "РїРЅРґ",
      "РІС‚": "РІС‚СЂ", 
      "СЃСЂ": "СЃСЂРґ",
      "С‡С‚": "С‡С‚РІ",
      "РїС‚": "РїС‚РЅ",
      "СЃР±": "СЃР±С‚",
      "РІСЃ": "РІСЃРє"
    };
    const shortWeekday = dayMap[weekday] || weekday;
    return `${day} ${month}, ${shortWeekday}`;
  };

  const formatReviewCount = (count: number) => {
    if (count >= 1000) {
      return `${(count / 1000).toFixed(1)}Рљ`;
    }
    return count.toString();
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
      <Header />
      <main className="container pt-24 pb-8 md:pt-32">
        {/* Р¤РѕСЂРјР° РїРѕРёСЃРєР° */}
        <Card className="mb-6">
          <CardContent className="p-6">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch">
                {/* РћС‚РєСѓРґР° */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РћС‚РєСѓРґР°</label>
                  <Select value={fromCity} onValueChange={(value) => {
                    setFromCity(value);
                    updateSearchParams(value, undefined);
                  }}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-12">
                      <SelectValue />
