                          { type: "comfort" as const, name: "РљРѕРјС„РѕСЂС‚" },
                          { type: "business" as const, name: "Р‘РёР·РЅРµСЃ" },
                          { type: "first" as const, name: "РџРµСЂРІС‹Р№ РєР»Р°СЃСЃ" }
                        ];

                        return allTicketTypes.map((ticketTypeInfo) => {
                          // РС‰РµРј Р±РёР»РµС‚ РІ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚Рµ
                          const ticket = route.tickets.find(t => t.type === ticketTypeInfo.type);
                          
                          // Р•СЃР»Рё Р±РёР»РµС‚Р° РЅРµС‚ РёР»Рё РѕРЅ СЂР°СЃРїСЂРѕРґР°РЅ
                          const isSoldOut = !ticket || ticket.seats === 0;
                          
                          return (
                            <div
                              key={ticketTypeInfo.type}
                              className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm"
                            >
                              <div>
                                <span className="font-medium">{ticketTypeInfo.name}</span>
                                {isSoldOut ? (

                                ) : (
                                  <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-2">
                                    {ticket.seats} РјРµСЃС‚
                                  </span>
                                )}
                              </div>
                              {!isSoldOut && ticket && (
                                <span className="font-semibold">
                                  РѕС‚ {ticket.price.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} Р 
                                </span>
                              )}

                        РѕС‚ {route.minPrice.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} Р

                            to: toCity,
                            date: format(departureDate, "yyyy-MM-dd"),
                            class: flightClass,
                            passengers: passengers,
                            travelType: "flight"
                          });

                      </Button>

        {routes.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center py-12">
            <p className="text-lg text-muted-foreground">

            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
};

export default FlightSearch;

interface Passenger {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  surname: string;
  patronymic?: string;
  passport_series: string;
  passport_number: string;

}

interface UserProfile {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  first_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  patronymic?: string;
  phone?: string;
  email?: string;
  birth_date?: string;
}

    order_status: "active" | "refunded" | "exchanged";

  interface Certificate {

    open: boolean;

      const ordersWithStatus = (data || []).map((order) => ({

        order_status: order.order_status || "active",

    } catch (error) {

        description: "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ Р·Р°РєР°Р·РѕРІ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });

    }

    if (!user) return;
    setLoadingCertificates(true);
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/rpc/expire_old_certificates", { method: "POST" });

      const data = await apiFetch<Certificate[]>("/api/certificates?all=1");
      setCertificates(data || []);
      setDisplayedCertificates(data || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error loading certificates:", error);
    } finally {
      setLoadingCertificates(false);
    }
  };

  // Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚РѕРІ
  useEffect(() => {
    if (certificateFilter === "all") {
      setDisplayedCertificates(certificates);
    } else {
      setDisplayedCertificates(certificates.filter(c => c.transport_type === certificateFilter));
    }
  }, [certificateFilter, certificates]);

  const handleConfirmRegistration = async (orderId: string) => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/tickets/${orderId}`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          electronic_registration_status: "confirmed",
        }),
      });

      toast({
        title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
        description: "Р­Р»РµРєС‚СЂРѕРЅРЅР°СЏ СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅР°",
      });
      setConfirmRegistrationModal({ open: false, orderId: null, routeInfo: "" });
      await loadTicketOrders();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error confirming registration:", error);
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂРґРёС‚СЊ СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёСЋ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRefundTicket = async (orderId: string, _orderNumber: string, totalPrice: number) => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const serviceFee = Math.round(totalPrice * 0.1);
      const refundAmount = totalPrice - serviceFee;

      const result = await apiFetch<{
        ok?: boolean;
        refund_amount?: string;
        already?: boolean;
      }>(`/api/tickets/${orderId}/refund`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ amount: refundAmount }),
      });

      const amountLabel = result.refund_amount
        ? Number(result.refund_amount).toLocaleString("ru-RU")
        : refundAmount.toLocaleString("ru-RU");

      toast({
        title: result.already ? "Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‚ СѓР¶Рµ РѕС„РѕСЂРјР»РµРЅ" : "Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‚ РѕС„РѕСЂРјР»РµРЅ",
        description: result.already
          ? "РџРѕ СЌС‚РѕРјСѓ Р·Р°РєР°Р·Сѓ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‚ СѓР¶Рµ Р±С‹Р» РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ СЂР°РЅРµРµ."
          : `Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‚ ${amountLabel} в‚Ѕ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅ С‡РµСЂРµР· Р®Kassa РЅР° РєР°СЂС‚Сѓ РѕРїР»Р°С‚С‹.`,
      });
      setRefundTicketModal({ open: false, orderId: "", orderNumber: "", totalPrice: 0, transportType: "" });
      await loadTicketOrders();
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      console.error("Error refunding ticket:", error);
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС„РѕСЂРјРёС‚СЊ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‚. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊС‚Рµ, С‡С‚Рѕ Р·Р°РєР°Р· РѕРїР»Р°С‡РµРЅ С‡РµСЂРµР· Р®Kassa.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const canRefundToCard = (order: TicketOrder) =>
    order.payment_status === "paid" && order.payment_method === "yookassa";

  const handleExchangeTicket = async (orderId: string, orderNumber: string, totalPrice: number, transportType: string) => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      console.log("Updating ticket to exchanged, orderId:", orderId);
      
      const ticketData = await apiFetch<Record<string, unknown> | null>(
        `/api/tickets/${orderId}`
      );

      if (!ticketData) {
        throw new Error("Р‘РёР»РµС‚ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ");
      }

      const serviceFee = Math.round(totalPrice * 0.1);
      const certificateAmount = totalPrice - serviceFee;

      const certificateData = await apiFetch<Record<string, unknown> | null>(
        "/api/rpc/create_certificate",
        {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({
            p_transport_type: transportType,
            p_amount: certificateAmount,
            p_order_id: orderId,
          }),
        }
      );

      if (!certificateData) {
        throw new Error("РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СЃРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚");
      }

      await apiFetch(`/api/tickets/${orderId}`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify({ order_status: "exchanged" }),
      });

      toast({
        title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
        description: `РЎРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅ РЅР° СЃСѓРјРјСѓ ${certificateAmount.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} в‚Ѕ. РџРѕСЃРјРѕС‚СЂРёС‚Рµ РµРіРѕ РІ РіСЂР°С„Рµ "РЎРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚С‹".`,
      });
      setExchangeTicketModal({ open: false, orderId: "", orderNumber: "", totalPrice: 0, transportType: "" });
      await loadTicketOrders();
      await loadCertificates(); // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚РѕРІ
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error("Error exchanging ticket:", error);
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: error?.message || "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°С‚СЊ РѕР±РјРµРЅ Р±РёР»РµС‚Р°. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊС‚Рµ РєРѕРЅСЃРѕР»СЊ Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂР°.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const getTransportTypeLabel = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case "train":
        return "РџРѕРµР·Рґ";
      case "flight":
        return "РЎР°РјРѕР»С‘С‚";
      case "bus":
        return "РђРІС‚РѕР±СѓСЃ";
      default:
        return type;
    }
  };

  const getTransportIcon = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case "train":
        return <Train className="w-4 h-4" />;
      case "flight":
        return <Plane className="w-4 h-4" />;
      case "bus":
        return <Bus className="w-4 h-4" />;
      default:
        return null;
    }
  };

  const getOrderStatus = (order: TicketOrder): "active" | "confirmed" | "refunded" | "exchanged" => {
    // Р•СЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ СЏРІРЅС‹Р№ СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‚Р° РёР»Рё РѕР±РјРµРЅР°
    if (order.order_status === "refunded") return "refunded";
    if (order.order_status === "exchanged") return "exchanged";
    
    // Р•СЃР»Рё СЌР»РµРєС‚СЂРѕРЅРЅР°СЏ СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅР°
    if (order.electronic_registration_status === "confirmed") return "confirmed";
    
    // РџРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ - Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ Р·Р°РєР°Р·
    return "active";
  };

  const getStatusConfig = (status: "active" | "confirmed" | "refunded" | "exchanged") => {
    switch (status) {
      case "active":
        return {
          label: "Р‘РёР»РµС‚ РєСѓРїР»РµРЅ",
          borderColor: "border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600",
          bgColor: "bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800/50",
          textColor: "text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300",
          icon: null,
        };
      case "confirmed":
        return {
          label: "Р­Р»РµРєС‚СЂРѕРЅРЅР°СЏ СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅР°",
          borderColor: "border-green-500 dark:border-green-600",
          bgColor: "bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20",
          textColor: "text-green-700 dark:text-green-400",
          icon: <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />,
        };
      case "refunded":
        return {
          label: "Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‚ СЃСЂРµРґСЃС‚РІ",
          borderColor: "border-red-500 dark:border-red-600",
          bgColor: "bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20",
          textColor: "text-red-700 dark:text-red-400",
          icon: <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />,
        };
      case "exchanged":
        return {
          label: "РћР±РјРµРЅ Р±РёР»РµС‚Р°",
          borderColor: "border-red-500 dark:border-red-600",
          bgColor: "bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20",
          textColor: "text-red-700 dark:text-red-400",
          icon: <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />,
        };
    }
  };

  const loadPassengers = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await apiFetch<Passenger[]>("/api/passengers");
      setPassengers(data || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error loading passengers:", error);
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleSavePassenger = async () => {
    if (!user) return;

    if (!formData.name || !formData.surname || !formData.passport_series || !formData.passport_number || !formData.birth_date) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: "Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РІСЃРµ РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЏ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    
    if (editingPassenger) {
      try {
        await apiFetch(`/api/passengers/${editingPassenger.id}`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          body: JSON.stringify({
            display_name: formData.display_name || null,
            name: formData.name,
            surname: formData.surname,
            patronymic: formData.patronymic || null,
            gender: formData.gender || null,
            passport_series: formData.passport_series,
            passport_number: formData.passport_number,
            birth_date: formData.birth_date,
          }),
        });
        toast({
          title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
          description: "РџР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅ",
        });
        setEditingPassenger(null);
        resetForm();
        loadPassengers();
      } catch {
        toast({
          title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
          description: "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
      }
    } else {
      try {
        await apiFetch("/api/passengers", {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({
            display_name: formData.display_name || null,
            name: formData.name,
            surname: formData.surname,
            patronymic: formData.patronymic || null,
            gender: formData.gender || null,
            passport_series: formData.passport_series,
            passport_number: formData.passport_number,
            birth_date: formData.birth_date,
          }),
        });
        toast({
          title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
          description: "РџР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅ",
        });
        resetForm();
        loadPassengers();
      } catch {
        toast({
          title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
          description: "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
      }
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleDeletePassenger = async (id: string) => {
    if (!confirm("Р’С‹ СѓРІРµСЂРµРЅС‹, С‡С‚Рѕ С…РѕС‚РёС‚Рµ СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°?")) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/passengers/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      toast({
        title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
        description: "РџР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂ СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅ",
      });
      loadPassengers();
    } catch {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleEditPassenger = (passenger: Passenger) => {
    setEditingPassenger(passenger);
    setFormData({
      display_name: passenger.display_name || "",
      name: passenger.name,
      surname: passenger.surname,
      patronymic: passenger.patronymic || "",
      gender: (passenger as any).gender || "",
      passport_series: passenger.passport_series,
      passport_number: passenger.passport_number,
      birth_date: passenger.birth_date,
    });
  };

  const resetForm = () => {
    setFormData({
      display_name: "",
      gender: "",
      name: "",
      surname: "",
      patronymic: "",
      passport_series: "",
      passport_number: "",
      birth_date: "",
    });
    setEditingPassenger(null);
  };

  const handleSaveProfile = async () => {
    if (!user) return;

    setLoading(true);
    
    // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅ РІ auth.users, РµСЃР»Рё РѕРЅ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅ
    // РќРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·СѓРµРј РЅРѕРјРµСЂ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР° РїРµСЂРµРґ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµРј
    let normalizedPhone = profileForm.phone;
    if (profileForm.phone) {
      const phoneDigits = profileForm.phone.replace(/\D/g, ''); // РЈР±РёСЂР°РµРј РІСЃРµ РєСЂРѕРјРµ С†РёС„СЂ
      
      // Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРѕРјРµСЂ РЅР°С‡РёРЅР°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃ 8, Р·Р°РјРµРЅСЏРµРј РЅР° +7
      if (phoneDigits.startsWith('8')) {
        normalizedPhone = '+7' + phoneDigits.substring(1);
      } else if (phoneDigits.startsWith('7')) {
        normalizedPhone = '+' + phoneDigits;
      } else {
        // Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРѕРјРµСЂ Р±РµР· РєРѕРґР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅС‹, РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј +7
        normalizedPhone = '+7' + phoneDigits;
      }
      
      console.log('Normalizing phone:', { original: profileForm.phone, normalized: normalizedPhone });
      profileForm.phone = normalizedPhone;
    }

    try {
      const patchBody: Record<string, string> = {};
      if (normalizedPhone) {
        patchBody.phone = normalizedPhone;
      }
      if (
        profileForm.email &&
        profileForm.email !== user.email &&
        !user.email?.includes("@temp.com")
      ) {
        patchBody.email = profileForm.email.trim().toLowerCase();
      }
      if (
        user.email?.includes("@temp.com") &&
        profileForm.email &&
        !profileForm.email.includes("@temp.com")
      ) {
        patchBody.email = profileForm.email.trim().toLowerCase();
      }
      if (Object.keys(patchBody).length > 0) {
        await apiFetch("/api/auth/me", {
          method: "PATCH",
          body: JSON.stringify(patchBody),
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Error updating auth user:", e);
    }
    
    // Р“РѕС‚РѕРІРёРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РґР»СЏ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ
    const profileData = {
      first_name: profileForm.first_name || null,
      last_name: profileForm.last_name || null,
      patronymic: profileForm.patronymic || null,
      phone: normalizedPhone || null,
      email: profileForm.email || user.email || null,
      birth_date: profileForm.birth_date || null,
    };
    
    console.log('Saving profile data:', profileData);

    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/user-profiles/me", {
        method: "PUT",
        body: JSON.stringify(profileData),
      });
      toast({
        title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
        description: "Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅС‹",
      });
      setShowProfileDialog(false);
      loadUserProfile();
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const err = error as Error;
      console.error("Error saving profile:", err);
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description:
          err.message || "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊС‚Рµ РєРѕРЅСЃРѕР»СЊ Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂР°.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleUpdatePassword = async () => {
    if (newPassword.length < 6) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: "РџР°СЂРѕР»СЊ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶Р°С‚СЊ РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј 6 СЃРёРјРІРѕР»РѕРІ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: "РџР°СЂРѕР»Рё РЅРµ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґР°СЋС‚",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    const { error } = await updatePassword(newPassword);
    setLoading(false);

    if (error) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description:
          (error as { message?: string })?.message ||
          "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РёР·РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ РїР°СЂРѕР»СЊ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } else {
      toast({
        title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
        description: "РџР°СЂРѕР»СЊ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅ",
      });
      setNewPassword("");
      setConfirmPassword("");
    }
  };

  const load2FAData = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    
    const data = await twoFactorAuth.get2FAData(user.id);
    setTwoFactorData(data);
  };

  const handleSetup2FA = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    
    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј, РµСЃС‚СЊ Р»Рё Сѓ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅРЅР°СЏ РїРѕС‡С‚Р°
    const userEmail = user.email;
    const profileEmail = userProfile?.email;
    
    // РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј email РґР»СЏ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІРєРё РєРѕРґР°
    const emailFor2FA = (userEmail && !userEmail.includes('@temp.com')) 
      ? userEmail 
      : (profileEmail && profileEmail.trim() !== '' ? profileEmail : null);
    
    if (!emailFor2FA) {
      toast({
        title: "РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°С‚СЊ email",
        description: "Р”Р»СЏ РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ РґРІСѓС…С„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРЅРѕР№ Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёРё РЅРµРѕР±С…РѕРґРёРјРѕ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°С‚СЊ email Рє Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚Сѓ. РџРѕР¶Р°Р»СѓР№СЃС‚Р°, РґРѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ email РІ Р»РёС‡РЅС‹С… РґР°РЅРЅС‹С….",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      setShowProfileDialog(true);
      return;
    }
    
    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј rate limit РґР»СЏ РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ 2FA
    const rateLimitCheck = await check2FARateLimit(user.id);
    if (!rateLimitCheck.allowed) {
      toast({
        title: "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚",
        description: rateLimitCheck.error || "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РѕРє РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ 2FA",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }
    
    // РћС‚РїСЂР°РІР»СЏРµРј РєРѕРґ РЅР° email РґР»СЏ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ 2FA
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/2fa/profile/send-email", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: emailFor2FA }),
      });
      setShow2FASetup(true);
      toast({
        title: "РљРѕРґ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅ",
        description: `РњС‹ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІРёР»Рё РєРѕРґ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ РЅР° ${emailFor2FA}`,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const err = error as Error;
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: err.message || "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІРёС‚СЊ РєРѕРґ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleEnable2FA = async () => {
    if (!user || twoFactorToken.length !== 6) return;
    
    // РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј email РґР»СЏ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё РєРѕРґР°
    const userEmail = user.email;
    const profileEmail = userProfile?.email;
    const emailFor2FA = (userEmail && !userEmail.includes('@temp.com')) 
      ? userEmail 
      : (profileEmail && profileEmail.trim() !== '' ? profileEmail : null);
    
    if (!emailFor2FA) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description: "Email РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }
    
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/2fa/profile/verify-email", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          email: emailFor2FA,
          code: twoFactorToken,
        }),
      });

      const result = await twoFactorAuth.enable(user.id);

      if (result.success) {
        resetRateLimit(`2fa_toggle:${user.id}`);
        toast({
          title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
          description: "Р”РІСѓС…С„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРЅР°СЏ Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёСЏ РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅР°",
        });
        setShow2FASetup(false);
        setTwoFactorToken("");
        await load2FAData();
      } else {
        toast({
          title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
          description: result.error || "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РІРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ 2FA",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
      }
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description:
          (error as Error).message || "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РІРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ 2FA",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleDisable2FA = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    
    if (!confirm("Р’С‹ СѓРІРµСЂРµРЅС‹, С‡С‚Рѕ С…РѕС‚РёС‚Рµ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РґРІСѓС…С„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРЅСѓСЋ Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёСЋ? Р­С‚Рѕ СЃРЅРёР·РёС‚ Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ РІР°С€РµРіРѕ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚Р°.")) {
      return;
    }
    
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await twoFactorAuth.disable(user.id);
      
      if (result.success) {
        toast({
          title: "РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ",
          description: "Р”РІСѓС…С„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРЅР°СЏ Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёСЏ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅР°",
        });
        await load2FAData();
      } else {
        toast({
          title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
          description: result.error || "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ 2FA",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
      }
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      toast({
        title: "РћС€РёР±РєР°",
        description:
          (error as Error).message || "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ 2FA",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  if (authLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
        <div>Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</div>
      </div>
    );

  if (!user) {
    return null;

            Р›РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ РєР°Р±РёРЅРµС‚

            Р’С‹Р№С‚Рё

                  Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ
                </div>

                  variant="outline"
                  size="sm"

                    // Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµРј С„РѕСЂРјСѓ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёРјРё РґР°РЅРЅС‹РјРё
                    const currentEmail = userProfile?.email || user?.email || "";
                    // РЈР±РёСЂР°РµРј @temp.com РёР· email РґР»СЏ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ, РµСЃР»Рё СЌС‚Рѕ РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ email
                    const displayEmail = currentEmail.replace('@temp.com', '');

                      first_name: userProfile?.first_name || "",

                  <p className="text-muted-foreground">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</p>

                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <Button onClick={handleSavePassenger} disabled={loading}>
                      {editingPassenger ? "РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ" : "РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°"}
                    </Button>
                    {editingPassenger && (
                      <Button onClick={resetForm} variant="outline">
                        РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
                      </Button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="border-t pt-6">
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-4">РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂС‹</h3>
                  {loading && passengers.length === 0 ? (
                    <p className="text-muted-foreground">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</p>
                  ) : passengers.length === 0 ? (
                    <p className="text-muted-foreground">РќРµС‚ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРЅС‹С… РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ</p>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      {passengers.map((passenger) => (
                        <Card key={passenger.id}>
                          <CardContent className="p-4">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                              <div>
                                <div className="font-medium">
                                  {passenger.display_name && (
                                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">({passenger.display_name}) </span>
                                  )}
                                  {passenger.surname} {passenger.name} {passenger.patronymic}
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                                  РџР°СЃРїРѕСЂС‚: {passenger.passport_series} {passenger.passport_number}
                                  {passenger.birth_date && ` вЂў Р”Р°С‚Р° СЂРѕР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ: ${new Date(passenger.birth_date).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}`}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex gap-2">
                                <Button
                                  onClick={() => handleEditPassenger(passenger)}
                                  variant="ghost"
                                  size="sm"
                                >
                                  <Edit className="w-4 h-4" />
                                </Button>
                                <Button
                                  onClick={() => handleDeletePassenger(passenger.id)}
                                  variant="ghost"
                                  size="sm"
                                >
                                  <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 text-destructive" />
                                </Button>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </CardContent>
                        </Card>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </TabsContent>

          <TabsContent value="certificates" className="mt-6">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Gift className="w-5 h-5" />
                  РЎРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚С‹
                </CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                {loadingCertificates ? (
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</p>
                ) : certificates.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground">РЈ РІР°СЃ РїРѕРєР° РЅРµС‚ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚РѕРІ</p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    {/* Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РїРѕ РєР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёСЏРј */}
                    <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      <Button
                        variant={certificateFilter === "all" ? "default" : "outline"}
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => setCertificateFilter("all")}
                      >
                        Р’СЃРµ
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        variant={certificateFilter === "train" ? "default" : "outline"}
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => setCertificateFilter("train")}
                      >
                        <Train className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                        РџРѕРµР·Рґ
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        variant={certificateFilter === "flight" ? "default" : "outline"}
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => setCertificateFilter("flight")}
                      >
                        <Plane className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                        РЎР°РјРѕР»С‘С‚
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        variant={certificateFilter === "bus" ? "default" : "outline"}
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => setCertificateFilter("bus")}
                      >
                        <Bus className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                        РђРІС‚РѕР±СѓСЃ
                      </Button>
                    </div>

                    {/* РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚РѕРІ */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      {displayedCertificates.length === 0 ? (
                        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-4">
                          РќРµС‚ СЃРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚РѕРІ РІ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅРѕР№ РєР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёРё
                        </p>
                      ) : (
                        displayedCertificates.map((cert) => {
                        const isExpired = new Date(cert.expires_at) < new Date();
                        const statusConfig = {
                          active: { label: "РђРєС‚РёРІРµРЅ", color: "text-green-600", bg: "bg-green-50", border: "border-green-200" },
                          used: { label: "РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅ", color: "text-gray-600", bg: "bg-gray-50", border: "border-gray-200" },
                          expired: { label: "РСЃС‚С‘Рє", color: "text-red-600", bg: "bg-red-50", border: "border-red-200" },
                        };
                        const config = statusConfig[cert.status] || statusConfig.active;
                        const transportIcon = cert.transport_type === "train" ? <Train className="w-4 h-4" /> :
                                             cert.transport_type === "flight" ? <Plane className="w-4 h-4" /> :
                                             <Bus className="w-4 h-4" />;
                        const transportLabel = cert.transport_type === "train" ? "РџРѕРµР·Рґ" :
                                              cert.transport_type === "flight" ? "РЎР°РјРѕР»С‘С‚" :
                                              "РђРІС‚РѕР±СѓСЃ";

                        return (
                          <Card key={cert.id} className={`${config.border} border-2`}>
                            <CardContent className="p-4">
                              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                                <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
                                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <Gift className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
                                    <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-lg">{cert.certificate_code}</span>
                                    <span className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium ${config.bg} ${config.color}`}>
                                      {config.label}
                                    </span>
                                  </div>
                                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                                    {transportIcon}
                                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">{transportLabel}</span>
                                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">|</span>
                                    <span className="font-semibold">{cert.amount.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} в‚Ѕ</span>
                                  </div>
                                  <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                                    РЎРѕР·РґР°РЅ: {new Date(cert.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
                                    {cert.used_at && (
                                      <> вЂў РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅ: {new Date(cert.used_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}</>
                                    )}
                                    {!cert.used_at && (
                                      <> вЂў РСЃС‚РµРєР°РµС‚: {new Date(cert.expires_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}</>
                                    )}
                                  </div>
                                  {cert.status === "active" && isExpired && (
                                    <div className="text-xs text-red-600">
                                      РЎРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚ РёСЃС‚С‘Рє Рё Р±СѓРґРµС‚ СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅ
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                  {cert.status === "used" && (
                                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                                      РЎРµСЂС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚ Р±СѓРґРµС‚ СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅ С‡РµСЂРµР· 24 С‡Р°СЃР° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </CardContent>
                          </Card>
                        );
                      })
                    )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </TabsContent>

          <TabsContent value="security" className="mt-6">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Shield className="w-5 h-5" />
                  Р‘РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ
                </CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-6">
                {/* Р”РІСѓС…С„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРЅР°СЏ Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёСЏ */}
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="font-semibold">Р”РІСѓС…С„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРЅР°СЏ Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёСЏ (2FA)</h3>
                      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                        Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅР°СЏ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚Р° РІР°С€РµРіРѕ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚Р°. РџСЂРё РІС…РѕРґРµ РїРѕ РїР°СЂРѕР»СЋ РїРѕС‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РґРѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РєРѕРґ СЃ РІР°С€РµР№ РїРѕС‡С‚С‹.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {twoFactorData?.enabled ? (
                        <span className="text-sm text-green-600 font-medium">Р’РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅР°</span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Р’С‹РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅР°</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {!twoFactorData?.enabled ? (
                    <div className="space-y-4">
                      {/* РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј РЅР°Р»РёС‡РёРµ email */}
                      {(() => {
                        const userEmail = user?.email;
                        const profileEmail = userProfile?.email;
                        // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј РЅР°Р»РёС‡РёРµ email (РµСЃР»Рё Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅ РїРѕ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅСѓ, email РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІРѕРІР°С‚СЊ)
                        const hasEmail = (userEmail && !userEmail.includes('@temp.com')) || 
                                         (profileEmail && profileEmail.trim() !== '');
                        
                        if (!hasEmail) {
                          return (
                            <div className="p-4 bg-yellow-50 dark:bg-yellow-900/20 border border-yellow-200 dark:border-yellow-800 rounded-lg">
                              <p className="text-sm text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-200 mb-2">
                                <strong>РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°С‚СЊ email</strong>
                              </p>
                              <p className="text-sm text-yellow-700 dark:text-yellow-300 mb-3">
                                Р”Р»СЏ РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ РґРІСѓС…С„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРЅРѕР№ Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёРё РЅРµРѕР±С…РѕРґРёРјРѕ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°С‚СЊ email Рє Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚Сѓ. 
                                РљРѕРґС‹ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ Р±СѓРґСѓС‚ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»СЏС‚СЊСЃСЏ РЅР° РІР°С€Сѓ РїРѕС‡С‚Сѓ.
                              </p>
                              <Button 
                                onClick={() => setShowProfileDialog(true)} 
                                variant="outline"
                                size="sm"
                              >
                                РџСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°С‚СЊ email
                              </Button>
                            </div>
                          );
                        }
                        
                        return (
                          <Button onClick={handleSetup2FA} disabled={loading}>
                            <Shield className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                            РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРёС‚СЊ 2FA
                          </Button>
                        );
                      })()}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="space-y-4">
                      <Button 
                        onClick={handleDisable2FA} 
                        variant="destructive" 
                        disabled={loading}
                      >
                        РћС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ 2FA
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Р”РёР°Р»РѕРі РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё 2FA */}
                <Dialog open={show2FASetup} onOpenChange={setShow2FASetup}>
                  <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[500px]">
                    <DialogHeader>
                      <DialogTitle>РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР° РґРІСѓС…С„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРЅРѕР№ Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёРё</DialogTitle>
                      <DialogDescription>
                        РњС‹ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІРёР»Рё РєРѕРґ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ РЅР° РІР°С€Сѓ РїРѕС‡С‚Сѓ. Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РєРѕРґ РґР»СЏ РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ 2FA.
                      </DialogDescription>
                    </DialogHeader>
                    <div className="space-y-4 py-4">
                      <div className="space-y-2">
                        <Label htmlFor="2fa_token">Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РєРѕРґ СЃ РїРѕС‡С‚С‹</Label>
                        <Input
                          id="2fa_token"
                          placeholder="000000"
                          value={twoFactorToken}
                          onChange={(e) => setTwoFactorToken(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 6))}
                          maxLength={6}
                        />
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          РљРѕРґ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅ РЅР° {userProfile?.email || user?.email}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex gap-2 justify-end">
                        <Button
                          variant="outline"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setShow2FASetup(false);
                            setTwoFactorToken("");
                          }}
                        >
                          РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
                        </Button>
                        <Button 
                          onClick={handleEnable2FA} 
                          disabled={loading || twoFactorToken.length !== 6}
                        >
                          Р’РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ 2FA
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </DialogContent>
                </Dialog>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </TabsContent>

          <TabsContent value="useful" className="mt-6">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>РџСЂРёРіРѕРґРёС‚СЃСЏ РІ РїРѕРµР·РґРєРµ</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <Button asChild>
                  <a href="http://localhost:8080/reference/trains" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                    РџРµСЂРµР№С‚Рё РЅР° СЃРїСЂР°РІРѕС‡РЅСѓСЋ
                    <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 ml-2" />
                  </a>
                </Button>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </TabsContent>

          <TabsContent value="contact" className="mt-6">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>РЎРІСЏР·Р°С‚СЊСЃСЏ СЃ РЅР°РјРё</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-4">
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Phone className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ: <a href="tel:+78001234567" className="text-primary hover:underline">+7 (800) 123-45-67</a></span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Mail className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>Email: <a href="mailto:info@ts-trip.ru" className="text-primary hover:underline">info@ts-trip.ru</a></span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>Telegram: <a href="https://t.me/tudasuda_support" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-primary hover:underline">@tudasuda_support</a></span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>WhatsApp: <a href="https://wa.me/78001234567" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-primary hover:underline">+7 (800) 123-45-67</a></span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </TabsContent>
        </Tabs>

        <Dialog open={showProfileDialog} onOpenChange={setShowProfileDialog}>
          <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[500px]">
            <DialogHeader>
              <DialogTitle>Р›РёС‡РЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ</DialogTitle>
              <DialogDescription>
                Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ РІР°С€Рё Р»РёС‡РЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ
              </DialogDescription>
            </DialogHeader>
            <div className="space-y-4 py-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="first_name">РРјСЏ</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="first_name"
                    value={profileForm.first_name}
                    onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, first_name: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="last_name">Р¤Р°РјРёР»РёСЏ</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="last_name"
                    value={profileForm.last_name}
                    onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, last_name: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="patronymic">РћС‚С‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="patronymic"
                    value={profileForm.patronymic}
                    onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, patronymic: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="birth_date">Р”Р°С‚Р° СЂРѕР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="birth_date"
                    type="date"
                    value={profileForm.birth_date}
                    onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, birth_date: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="phone">РўРµР»РµС„РѕРЅ</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="phone"
                    type="tel"
                    placeholder="+79001234567"
                    value={profileForm.phone}
                    onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, phone: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="email"
                    type="email"
                    value={profileForm.email}
                    onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, email: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2 justify-end">
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={() => setShowProfileDialog(false)}
                >
                  РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
                </Button>
                <Button onClick={handleSaveProfile} disabled={loading}>
                  РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </DialogContent>
        </Dialog>

        {/* РњРѕРґР°Р»РєР° РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ СЌР»РµРєС‚СЂРѕРЅРЅРѕР№ СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёРё */}
        <ConfirmRegistrationModal
          open={confirmRegistrationModal.open}
          onClose={() => {
            setConfirmRegistrationModal({ open: false, orderId: null, routeInfo: "" });
          }}
          onConfirm={() => {
            if (confirmRegistrationModal.orderId) {
              handleConfirmRegistration(confirmRegistrationModal.orderId);
            }
          }}
          routeInfo={confirmRegistrationModal.routeInfo}
        />

        {/* РњРѕРґР°Р»РєР° СЃРєР°С‡РёРІР°РЅРёСЏ Р±РёР»РµС‚РѕРІ */}
        <DownloadTicketModal
          open={downloadTicketModal.open}
          onClose={() => {
            setDownloadTicketModal({ open: false, tickets: [] });
          }}
          tickets={downloadTicketModal.tickets}
        />

        {/* РњРѕРґР°Р»РєР° РѕР±РјРµРЅР° Р±РёР»РµС‚Р° */}
        <ExchangeTicketModal
          open={exchangeTicketModal.open}
          onClose={() => {
            setExchangeTicketModal({ open: false, orderId: "", orderNumber: "", totalPrice: 0, transportType: "" });
          }}
          onConfirm={() => {
            if (exchangeTicketModal.orderId) {
              handleExchangeTicket(
                exchangeTicketModal.orderId,
                exchangeTicketModal.orderNumber,
                exchangeTicketModal.totalPrice,
                exchangeTicketModal.transportType
              );
            }
          }}
          orderNumber={exchangeTicketModal.orderNumber}
          totalPrice={exchangeTicketModal.totalPrice}
        />

        {/* РњРѕРґР°Р»РєР° РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‚Р° РґРµРЅРµРі */}
        <RefundTicketModal
          open={refundTicketModal.open}
          onClose={() => {
            if (!loading) {
              setRefundTicketModal({
                open: false,
                orderId: "",
                orderNumber: "",
                totalPrice: 0,
                transportType: "",
              });
            }
          }}
          onConfirm={() => {
            if (refundTicketModal.orderId) {
              void handleRefundTicket(
                refundTicketModal.orderId,
                refundTicketModal.orderNumber,
                refundTicketModal.totalPrice
              );
            }
          }}
          orderNumber={refundTicketModal.orderNumber}
          totalPrice={refundTicketModal.totalPrice}
          isProcessing={loading}
        />
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
};

export default Profile;

const SelectSeats = () => {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const routeId = searchParams.get("routeId");
  const fromCity = searchParams.get("from") || "РњРѕСЃРєРІР°";
  const toCity = searchParams.get("to") || "РЎР°РЅРєС‚-РџРµС‚РµСЂР±СѓСЂРі";
  const dateStr = searchParams.get("date");
  const ticketType = searchParams.get("ticketType") || "coupe";

  const travelType = searchParams.get("travelType") || "train"; // train, flight, bus

  const initialAdults = parseInt(passengersParam) >= 2 && parseInt(passengersParam) <= 4 
    ? parseInt(passengersParam) 
    : 1;

  const [adults, setAdults] = useState(initialAdults);
  const [children, setChildren] = useState(0);
  const [infants, setInfants] = useState(0);
  const [selectedTicketType, setSelectedTicketType] = useState<string>(
    travelType === "flight" ? flightClass : ticketType
  );
  const [busSeatFilter, setBusSeatFilter] = useState<"all" | "window">("all"); // Р”Р»СЏ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°С†РёРё РјРµСЃС‚ РІ Р°РІС‚РѕР±СѓСЃР°С…
  
  // РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј, РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ Р»Рё РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°С‚СЊ РіСЂР°С„Сѓ РґР»СЏ РјР°Р»С‹С€РµР№ РґРѕ 5 Р»РµС‚
  const showInfants = travelType === "train";

  const getFilteredBestOptions = (): BestOption[] => {
    let options: BestOption[] = [];
    
    if (travelType === "train") {
      options = trainBestOptions.filter(opt => opt.ticketType === selectedTicketType);
      // Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРµС‚ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚РѕРІ РґР»СЏ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ С‚РёРїР°, РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј СЃР°РјРѕРµ РґРµС€С‘РІРѕРµ
      if (options.length === 0) {
        options = trainBestOptions.sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price).slice(0, 3);
      }
    } else if (travelType === "flight") {
      options = flightBestOptions.filter(opt => opt.ticketType === selectedTicketType);
      // Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРµС‚ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚РѕРІ РґР»СЏ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ С‚РёРїР°, РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј СЃР°РјРѕРµ РґРµС€С‘РІРѕРµ
      if (options.length === 0) {
        options = flightBestOptions.sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price).slice(0, 3);
      }
    } else if (travelType === "bus") {
      // Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂСѓРµРј РїРѕ С‚РёРїСѓ РјРµСЃС‚Р° (РІСЃРµ РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅС‹Рµ РёР»Рё Сѓ РѕРєРЅР°)
      if (busSeatFilter === "window") {
        options = busBestOptions.filter(opt => opt.isWindow === true);
      } else {
        options = busBestOptions;
      }
      // Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРµС‚ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚РѕРІ РґР»СЏ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°, РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј РІСЃРµ
      if (options.length === 0) {
        options = busBestOptions;
      }
    }
    
    return options.slice(0, 3); // РџРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј 3 РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚Р°

      window: "РЈ РѕРєРЅР°"

    return names[amenity] || amenity;

  const handleSelectOption = (option: BestOption) => {
    const totalPrice = option.price * totalPassengers;
    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      routeId: routeId || "",
      from: fromCity,

      date: dateStr || format(departureDate, "yyyy-MM-dd"),
      carNumber: option.carNumber,
      seatNumber: option.seatNumber,

      price: totalPrice.toString(),
      adults: adults.toString(),

      infants: infants.toString()
    });
    navigate(`/checkout?${params.toString()}`);
  };

              <div className="space-y-3">

"РљРѕРјС„РѕСЂС‚" : "РџРµСЂРІС‹Р№ РєР»Р°СЃСЃ"}, {totalPassengers} {totalPassengers === 1 ? "РјРµСЃС‚Рѕ" : totalPassengers < 5 ? "РјРµСЃС‚Р°" : "РјРµСЃС‚"}

                                </>
                              )}
                              {travelType === "flight" && (

                                  РњРµСЃС‚Рѕ {option.seatNumber}, СЃРµРєС†РёСЏ {option.carNumber}

interface Amenity {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  name: string;
  description: string;
}

interface TicketType {
  type: "platzkart" | "coupe" | "sv";
  name: string;
  lowerBerths: number;
  upperBerths: number;
  price: number;
}

interface TrainRoute {
  id: string;
  trainNumber: string;
  trainName: string;
  tags?: string[];
  departureTime: string;
  departureDate: Date;
  departureStation: string;
  departureCity: string;

    count: number;
    rating: number;
  };
  tickets: TicketType[];
  minPrice: number;
}

/** Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃ Р±СЌРєРµРЅРґР° (РґР°С‚С‹ Р±РµР· РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРё, ISO-РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЊ) */
interface TrainRouteFromApi
  extends Omit<TrainRoute, "departureDate" | "arrivalDate" | "amenities"> {
  departureDate: string;
  arrivalDate: string;
}

const RZD_FALLBACK_AMENITIES = amenitiesData.slice(0, 5);

function deserializeTrainRoutes(rows: TrainRouteFromApi[]): TrainRoute[] {
  const ref = new Date();
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    ...r,
    departureDate: parse(r.departureDate, "yyyy-MM-dd", ref),
    arrivalDate: parse(r.arrivalDate, "yyyy-MM-dd", ref),
    amenities: RZD_FALLBACK_AMENITIES,
  }));
}

function filterRoutesByTicketType(
  routes: TrainRoute[],
  ticketType: string
): TrainRoute[] {
  if (!ticketType || ticketType === "all") return routes;
  return routes.filter((route) =>
    route.tickets.some((ticket) => ticket.type === ticketType)
  );
}

const TrainSearchResults = () => {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const initialFromCity = searchParams.get("from") || "РњРѕСЃРєРІР°";
  const initialToCity = searchParams.get("to") || "РЎР°РЅРєС‚-РџРµС‚РµСЂР±СѓСЂРі";
  const dateStr = searchParams.get("date");
  const initialTicketType = searchParams.get("ticketType") || "all";
  const initialPassengers = searchParams.get("passengers") || "1";

  const [fromCity, setFromCity] = useState(initialFromCity);
  const [toCity, setToCity] = useState(initialToCity);
  const [ticketType, setTicketType] = useState(initialTicketType);
  const [passengers, setPassengers] = useState(initialPassengers);
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState<Date>(dateStr ? new Date(dateStr) : new Date());

  const departureDate = selectedDate;

  const [rawRoutes, setRawRoutes] = useState<TrainRouteFromApi[]>([]);
  const [routeLoad, setRouteLoad] = useState<"idle" | "loading" | "error" | "ok">("idle");
  const [routeError, setRouteError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const normalizedRoutes = useMemo(
    () => deserializeTrainRoutes(rawRoutes),
    [rawRoutes]
  );
  const routes = useMemo(
    () => filterRoutesByTicketType(normalizedRoutes, ticketType),
    [normalizedRoutes, ticketType]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    const day = format(departureDate, "yyyy-MM-dd");
    setRouteLoad("loading");
    setRouteError(null);
    (async () => {
      try {
        const data = await apiFetch<{ routes: TrainRouteFromApi[]; message?: string }>(
          `/api/train-search/rzd?from=${encodeURIComponent(fromCity)}&to=${encodeURIComponent(toCity)}&date=${encodeURIComponent(day)}`
        );
        if (cancelled) return;
        setRawRoutes(Array.isArray(data.routes) ? data.routes : []);
        setRouteLoad("ok");
        if (data.message && (!data.routes || data.routes.length === 0)) {
          setRouteError(data.message);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setRawRoutes([]);
        setRouteLoad("error");
        setRouteError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ СЂР°СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ");
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [fromCity, toCity, departureDate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [fromCity, toCity, selectedDate, ticketType]);

  // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј URL РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРІ
  const updateSearchParams = (newFrom?: string, newTo?: string, newDate?: Date, newTicketType?: string, newPassengers?: string) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set("from", newFrom || fromCity);
    params.set("to", newTo || toCity);
    params.set("date", format(newDate || selectedDate, "yyyy-MM-dd"));
    if (newTicketType !== undefined) {
      params.set("ticketType", newTicketType || "all");
    } else if (ticketType !== "all") {
      params.set("ticketType", ticketType);
    }
    params.set("passengers", newPassengers || passengers);
    setSearchParams(params);
  };

  const handleSwapCities = () => {
    const newFrom = toCity;
    const newTo = fromCity;
    setFromCity(newFrom);
    setToCity(newTo);
    updateSearchParams(newFrom, newTo);
  };

  const handleDateChange = (date: Date) => {
    setSelectedDate(date);
    updateSearchParams(undefined, undefined, date);
  };

  const handleSearch = () => {
    updateSearchParams();
  };

  // Р“РµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј РґР°С‚С‹ РґР»СЏ РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЏ (РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅР°СЏ + 2 РґРѕ/РїРѕСЃР»Рµ)
  const getDateRange = () => {
    const dates: Date[] = [];
    for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {
      dates.push(addDays(selectedDate, i));
    }
    return dates;
  };

  const dateRange = getDateRange();

  const formatDate = (date: Date) => {
    return format(date, "d MMM, EEE", { locale: ru });
  };

  const formatShortDate = (date: Date) => {
    const day = format(date, "d", { locale: ru });
    const month = format(date, "MMM", { locale: ru });
    const weekday = format(date, "EEE", { locale: ru });
    // РЎРѕРєСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ РґРЅРµР№ РЅРµРґРµР»Рё РґРѕ 3 Р±СѓРєРІ
    const dayMap: Record<string, string> = {
      "РїРЅ": "РїРЅРґ",
      "РІС‚": "РІС‚СЂ", 
      "СЃСЂ": "СЃСЂРґ",
      "С‡С‚": "С‡С‚РІ",
      "РїС‚": "РїС‚РЅ",
      "СЃР±": "СЃР±С‚",
      "РІСЃ": "РІСЃРє"
    };
    const shortWeekday = dayMap[weekday] || weekday;
    return `${day} ${month}, ${shortWeekday}`;
  };

  const formatReviewCount = (count: number) => {
    if (count >= 1000) {
      return `${(count / 1000).toFixed(1)}Рљ`;
    }
    return count.toString();
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
      <Header />
      <main className="container pt-24 pb-8 md:pt-32">
        {/* Р¤РѕСЂРјР° РїРѕРёСЃРєР° */}
        <Card className="mb-6">
          <CardContent className="p-6">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch">
                {/* РћС‚РєСѓРґР° */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РћС‚РєСѓРґР°</label>
                  <Select value={fromCity} onValueChange={(value) => {
                    setFromCity(value);
                    updateSearchParams(value, undefined);
                  }}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-12">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      {cities.map((city) => (
                        <SelectItem key={`from-${city}`} value={city}>
                          {city}
                        </SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                {/* РљРЅРѕРїРєР° РѕР±РјРµРЅР° */}
                <div className="flex items-end">
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="icon"
                    className="h-12 w-12"
                    onClick={handleSwapCities}
                  >
                    <ArrowLeftRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </Button>
                </div>

                {/* РљСѓРґР° */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РљСѓРґР°</label>
                  <Select value={toCity} onValueChange={(value) => {
                    setToCity(value);
                    updateSearchParams(undefined, value);
                  }}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-12">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      {cities.map((city) => (
                        <SelectItem key={`to-${city}`} value={city}>
                          {city}
                        </SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                {/* РљРѕРіРґР° */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РљРѕРіРґР°</label>
                  <Popover>
                    <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                      <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        className={cn(
                          "h-12 w-full justify-start text-left font-normal px-3",
                          "text-sm"
                        )}
                      >
                        <CalendarIcon className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 text-muted-foreground" />
                        {formatDate(selectedDate)}
                      </Button>
                    </PopoverTrigger>
                    <PopoverContent className="w-auto p-0" align="start">
                      <Calendar
                        mode="single"
                        selected={selectedDate}
                        onSelect={(date) => {
                          if (date) {
                            handleDateChange(date);
                          }
                        }}
                        initialFocus
                        numberOfMonths={2}
                        disabled={(date) => date < new Date()}
                        className="pointer-events-auto"
                      />
                    </PopoverContent>
                  </Popover>
                </div>

                {/* РљС‚Рѕ РµРґРµС‚ */}
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1 block">РљС‚Рѕ РµРґРµС‚</label>
                  <Select value={passengers} onValueChange={(value) => {
                    setPassengers(value);
                    updateSearchParams(undefined, undefined, undefined, undefined, value);
                  }}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-12">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="1">1 РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂ</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="2">2 РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="3">3 РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="4">4 РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂР°</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                {/* РљРЅРѕРїРєР° РїРѕРёСЃРєР° */}
                <div className="flex items-end">
                  <Button
                    className="h-12 px-8 bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700"
                    onClick={handleSearch}
                  >
                    РќР°Р№С‚Рё РїРѕРµР·РґР°
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* РљР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЊ СЃ РґР°С‚Р°РјРё */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-4">
                <Popover>
                  <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                    <button className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm flex-shrink-0 border border-purple-200 hover:bg-purple-50 transition-colors">
                      <CalendarIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-600" />
                    </button>
                  </PopoverTrigger>
                  <PopoverContent className="w-auto p-0" align="start">
                    <Calendar
                      mode="single"
                      selected={selectedDate}
                      onSelect={(date) => {
                        if (date) {
                          handleDateChange(date);
                        }
                      }}
                      initialFocus
                      numberOfMonths={2}
                      disabled={(date) => date < new Date()}
                      className="pointer-events-auto"
                    />
                  </PopoverContent>
                </Popover>
                <div className="flex gap-2 flex-1 overflow-x-auto">
                  {dateRange.map((date, index) => {
                    const isSelected = format(date, "yyyy-MM-dd") === format(selectedDate, "yyyy-MM-dd");
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={index}
                        onClick={() => handleDateChange(date)}
                        className={cn(
                          "px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all flex-shrink-0",
                          isSelected
                            ? "bg-purple-600 text-white shadow-md"
                            : "bg-white border border-purple-200 text-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-50 hover:border-purple-300"
                        )}
                      >
                        <div className="font-semibold">{formatShortDate(date)}</div>
                        <div className={cn(
                          "text-xs mt-1",
                          isSelected ? "text-white/90" : "text-muted-foreground"
                        )}>
                          {routeLoad === "loading"
                            ? "вЂ¦"
                            : normalizedRoutes[0]?.minPrice
                              ? `${normalizedRoutes[0].minPrice.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} Р `
                              : "вЂ”"}
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {/* Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-8">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Train className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
          </div>
          <h1 className="heading-gradient text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            Р–/Рґ Р±РёР»РµС‚С‹
          </h1>
        </div>

        {/* РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚РѕРІ */}
        {routeLoad === "loading" && (
          <p className="text-center py-16 text-muted-foreground">
            Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј СЂР°СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ Р Р–Р”вЂ¦
          </p>
        )}
        {routeLoad === "error" && routeError && (
          <p className="text-center py-8 text-red-600" role="alert">
            {routeError}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {routeLoad !== "loading" &&
          routes.map((route) => (
            <Card key={route.id} className="hover:shadow-lg transition-shadow">
              <CardContent className="p-6">
                <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-6">
                  {/* Р›РµРІР°СЏ С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ: РРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ Рѕ РїРѕРµР·РґРµ */}
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-4">
                    {/* РўРµРіРё Рё РЅРѕРјРµСЂ РїРѕРµР·РґР° */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      {route.tags?.map((tag) => (
                        <span
                          key={tag}
                          className="px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full bg-purple-100 text-purple-900"
                        >
                          {tag}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                      <span className="px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full bg-muted text-muted-foreground">
                        {route.trainNumber} {route.trainName}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Р’СЂРµРјСЏ Рё СЃС‚Р°РЅС†РёРё */}
                    <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-6 items-start md:items-center">
                      {/* РћС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ */}
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-3xl font-bold">{route.departureTime}</div>
                          <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                            {formatDate(route.departureDate)}
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-sm font-medium mt-1">{route.departureStation}</div>
                          <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{route.departureCity}</div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* РџСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃ Р»РёРЅРёРµР№ */}
                      <div className="flex-1 relative">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-2">
                          <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
                          <span>
                            {route.duration === "вЂ”"
                              ? route.duration
                              : `${route.duration} РІ РїСѓС‚Рё`}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="relative h-[2px] bg-border">
                          <div className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary" />
                          <div className="absolute right-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary" />
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* РџСЂРёР±С‹С‚РёРµ */}
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-3xl font-bold">{route.arrivalTime}</div>
                          <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                            {formatDate(route.arrivalDate)}
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-sm font-medium mt-1">{route.arrivalStation}</div>
                          <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{route.arrivalCity}</div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* РЈРґРѕР±СЃС‚РІР° */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      <TooltipProvider delayDuration={0}>
                        {route.amenities.map((amenity, index) => (
                          <Tooltip key={index}>
                            <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                              <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 cursor-help transition-colors border border-purple-100">
                                <div className="text-purple-600">
                                  {amenity.icon}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </TooltipTrigger>
                            <TooltipContent side="top" className="bg-[#1a1a1a] text-white border-0">
                              <div className="space-y-1">
                                <div className="font-semibold text-white">{amenity.name}</div>
                                <div className="text-sm text-gray-300 max-w-xs">
                                  {amenity.description}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </TooltipContent>
                          </Tooltip>
                        ))}
                      </TooltipProvider>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* РџСЂР°РІР°СЏ С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ: РћС‚Р·С‹РІС‹, С†РµРЅС‹ Рё РєРЅРѕРїРєР° */}
                  <div className="lg:w-80 space-y-4">
                    {/* РћС‚Р·С‹РІС‹ */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                      {route.reviews.count > 0 ? (
                        <>
                          <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                            {formatReviewCount(route.reviews.count)} РѕС‚Р·С‹РІРѕРІ
                          </span>
                          <div className="px-2 py-1 bg-green-100 text-green-800 rounded text-sm font-semibold">
                            {route.reviews.rating}
                          </div>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                          РћС†РµРЅРєРё РѕС‚ РїР°СЃСЃР°Р¶РёСЂРѕРІ РЅРµ СЃРѕР±РёСЂР°РµРј
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* РўРёРїС‹ Р±РёР»РµС‚РѕРІ Рё С†РµРЅС‹ */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      {(() => {
                        // Р’СЃРµ РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅС‹Рµ С‚РёРїС‹ РјРµСЃС‚
                        const allTicketTypes = [
                          { type: "platzkart" as const, name: "РџР»Р°С†РєР°СЂС‚" },
                          { type: "coupe" as const, name: "РљСѓРїРµ" },
                          { type: "sv" as const, name: "РЎР’" }
                        ];

                        return allTicketTypes.map((ticketTypeInfo) => {
                          // РС‰РµРј Р±РёР»РµС‚ РІ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚Рµ
                          const ticket = route.tickets.find(t => t.type === ticketTypeInfo.type);
                          
                          // Р•СЃР»Рё Р±РёР»РµС‚Р° РЅРµС‚ РёР»Рё РѕРЅ СЂР°СЃРїСЂРѕРґР°РЅ
                          const isSoldOut = !ticket || (ticket.lowerBerths === 0 && ticket.upperBerths === 0);
                          
                          return (
                            <div
                              key={ticketTypeInfo.type}
                              className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm"
                            >
                              <div>
                                <span className="font-medium">{ticketTypeInfo.name}</span>
                                {isSoldOut ? (
                                  <span className="text-red-600 ml-2 font-semibold">Р Р°СЃРїСЂРѕРґР°РЅРѕ</span>
                                ) : (
                                  <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-2">
                                    {ticket.lowerBerths > 0 && `${ticket.lowerBerths} РЅРёР¶`}
                                    {ticket.lowerBerths > 0 && ticket.upperBerths > 0 && ", "}
                                    {ticket.upperBerths > 0 && `${ticket.upperBerths} РІРµСЂС…`}
                                  </span>
                                )}
                              </div>
                              {!isSoldOut && ticket && (
                                <span className="font-semibold">
                                  РѕС‚ {ticket.price.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} Р 
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          );
                        });
                      })()}
                    </div>

                    {/* Р¦РµРЅР° Рё РєРЅРѕРїРєР° */}
                    <div className="pt-4 border-t">
                      <div className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1">
                        {route.minPrice > 0 ? (
                          <>РѕС‚ {route.minPrice.toLocaleString("ru-RU")} Р </>
                        ) : (
                          <>С†РµРЅР° РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅР°</>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-4">Р·Р° РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ</div>
                      <Button
                        className="w-full bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700"
                        onClick={() => {
                          const params = new URLSearchParams({
                            routeId: route.id,
                            from: fromCity,
                            to: toCity,
                            date: format(departureDate, "yyyy-MM-dd"),
                            ticketType: ticketType,
                            passengers: passengers,
                            travelType: "train"
                          });
                          navigate(`/select-seats?${params.toString()}`);
                        }}
                      >
                        Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ РјРµСЃС‚Р°
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>

        {routeLoad === "ok" && routes.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center py-12">
            <p className="text-lg text-muted-foreground">
              {routeError ||
                "Рљ СЃРѕР¶Р°Р»РµРЅРёСЋ, РЅР° РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅСѓСЋ РґР°С‚Сѓ РЅРµС‚ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґСЏС‰РёС… РїРѕРµР·РґРѕРІ (РёР·РјРµРЅРёС‚Рµ С‚РёРї РјРµСЃС‚ РёР»Рё РґР°С‚Сѓ)."}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
};

export default TrainSearchResults;

/** Р‘Р»РѕРєРё С‚РµР»Р° СЃС‚Р°С‚СЊРё (С…СЂР°РЅСЏС‚СЃСЏ РІ blog_posts.content_blocks, JSONB). */

export type RouteTab = {

  blocks: BlogContentBlock[];
};

export type BlogCarouselSlide = {
  image: string;
  caption?: string;
  badge?: string;
  discount?: string;
  route?: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  price?: string;
  oldPrice?: string;
  alt?: string;
};

export type BlogCarouselMode = "manual" | "auto" | "hybrid";

export type BlogTableCell = { text: string };
